1b2(1): byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das gzhal med khang rin po che sna bdun mchog tu 'bar ba bkod pa 'jig rten
1b3(1): gyi khams dpag tu med pa rgyas par 'gengs pa'i 'od zer chen po shin tu mnga' ba/gnas kyi bye brag shin tu rnam par phye ba/mtha' yas pa rnam par bzhag pa/dkyil 'khor yongs su
1b4(1): ma chad pa/khams gsum pa las yang dag par 'das pa'i spyod yul/'jig rten las 'das pa de'i bla ma'i dge ba'i rtsa ba las byung ba/dbang sgyur ba'i rnam par rig pa shin tu rnam par
1b5(1): rig pa'i mtshan nyid/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gnas/byang chub sems dpa' dpag tu med pa'i dge 'dun rjes su dong ba/lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang dri za dang lha ma yin dang nam
1b6(1): mkha' lding dang mi 'am ci dang lto 'phye chen po dang mi dang mi ma yin pa mtha' yas pa rnam par rgyu ba/chos kyi ro'i dga' ba dang bde ba chen pos brtan pa/sems can thams cad kyi don thams cad yang dag
1b7(1): par rton par mdzad pas nye bar gnas pa/nyon mongs pa'i dri ma'i gnod pa thams cad dang bral ba/bdud thams cad yongs su spangs pa/thams cad kyi bkod pa las lhag pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i
1b8(1): byin gyi brlab kyis bkod pa//dran pa dang blo gros dang /rtogs pa chen pos nges par 'byung ba/zhi gnas dang lhag mthong chen po bzhon pa yin pa/rnam par thar pa'i sgo chen po stong pa nyid dang mtshan ma med pa
1b9(1): dang /smon pa med pa nas 'jug pa/rin po che'i pad+ma'i rgyal po chen po yon tan gyi tshogs mtha' yas pas brgyan pa'i bkod pa la brten pa na bzhugs te/bcom ldan 'das legs par thugs su chud pa'i blo
2a1(1): @#//dang ldan pa/kun tu spyod pa/gnyis mi mnga' ba/mtshan nyid med pa'i chos la mchog tu gzhol bar mdzad pa/sangs rgyas kyi gnas pas gnas pa/sangs rgyas 
2a2(1): thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid brnyes pa/sgrib pa med pa'i rtogs par thugs su chud pa/phyir mi ldog pa'i chos dang ldan pa/spyod yul gyis mi 'phrogs pa/rnam par gzhag pa/bsam 
2a3(1): gyis mi khyab pa rnam par 'jog pa/dus gsum la mnyam pa nyid tshar phyin pa/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du zhugs pa'i sku lnga dang ldan pa/chos thams cad la the tshom med pa'i mkhyen pa mnga' ba/
2a4(1): spyod pa thams cad dang ldan pa'i blo mnga' ba/chos shes pa la nem nur mi mnga' ba/rnam par ma brtags pa'i sku mnga' ba/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis ye shes yang dag par 
2a5(1): blangs pa/sangs rgyas kyi gnasapa gnyis su med pa/dam pa'i pha rol tu byon pa/de bzhin gshegs pa ma 'dres pa'i rnam par thar par mdzad pa'i ye shes kyi mthar phyin pa/mtha' dang dpung 
2a6(1): med pa'i sangs rgyas kyis mnyam pa nyid thugs su chud pa/chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/nam mkha'i khams kyi mthas gtugs pa de/nyan thos kyi dge 'dun tshad med pa thams cad kyang cang 
2a7(1): shes pa/sangs rgyas kyi sras/sangs rgyas kyi sras/

2a9(1): byed pa/shes rab myur ba/shes rab mgyogs pa/shes rab rno ba/nges par 'byung ba'i shes rab can/nges par rtogs pa'i shes rab can/shes rab che ba/shes 
2a10(1): rab yangs pa/shes rab zab pa/shes rab mnyam pa med pa/shes rab rin po che dang ldan pa/rig pa gsum dang ldan pa/tshe 'di la bde bar gnas pa'i mchog thob pa/yongs su sbyong ba chen 
2b1(1): bo/spyod lam rab tu zhi ba phun sum tshogs pa/bzod pa dang des pa chen po dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' la shin tu zhugs pa sha stag dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing tha dad pa nas 'dus pa'i byang chub 
2b2(1): sems dpa' dpag tu med pa thams cad kyang gnas chen po la gnas shing yang dag par zhugs pa/theg pa chen po'i chos kyis nges par byung ba/sems can thams cad la sems mnyam pa/bskal pa dang bskal pa 
2b3(1): ma yin par rnam par rtogs pa thams cad bral ba/bdud dang phyir rgol ba thams cad bcom pa/nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi yid la byed pa thams cad las ring du gyur pa/chos kyi ro'i dga' ba dang /bde 
2b4(1): ba chen pos brtan pa/'jigs pa chen po lnga las yang dag par 'das pa/phyir mi ldog pa'i sa bgrod pa gcig par gyur ro//sems can thams cad kyi gnod pa thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa'i sa mngon du gyur 
2b5(1): pa sha stag la 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po don zab dgongs pa nges par 'grel dang /tshul bzhin kun 'dr-i dang /chos 'phags dang /blo gros shin tu rnam dag dang /blo gros yangs pa 
2b6(1): dang /yon tan 'byung gnas dang /don dam yang dag 'phags dang /'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug dang /byams pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chen po 'jam 
2b7(1): dpal la sogs pa dang thabs cig go/de nas byang chub sems dpa' tshul bzhin kun 'dris kyis brjod du med pa dang /gnyis su med pa'i mtshan nyid don dam pa las brtsams te/byang chub sems dpa' don zab 
2b8(1): dgongs pa nges par 'grelapa la dris pa dris pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras chos thams cad gnyis su med pa chos thams cad gnyis su ma med pa zhes bya na/chos thams cad ni gang /ji ltar na gnyis su med 
2b9(1): pa yin/rigs kyi bu chos thams cad chos thams cad ces bya ba ni gnyis ji tsam pa ste/'dus byas dang /'dus ma byas so//de la 'dus byas ni 'dus byas kyang ma yin/'dus 
2b10(1): ma byas kyang ma yin no//'dus ma byas ni 'dus ma byas kyang ma yin/'dus byas kyang ma yin no/smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras ji ltar na 'dus byas ni 'dus byas/kyang ma yin/'dus ma byas kyang 
3a1(1): ma yin la/'dus ma byas kyang 'dus ma byas kyang ma yin/'dus ma byas kyang ma yin/rigs kyi bu 'dus byas zhes kyang de ni ston pas btags pa'i tshig yin te/ston pas btags 
3a2(1): pa'i tshig gang yin pa de ni/kun tu rtog pa las byung ba tha snyad du brjod pa yin la/kun tu rtog pa las 'byung ba tha snyad du brjod gang yin pa de ni/ku tu rtog pa sna tshogs kyi tha snyad du brjod pa gtan yongs su ma grub pa'i phyir 'dus
3a3(1): byas ma yin no//rigs kyi bu 'dus ma byas zhes bya ba de'ang tha snyad kyi khongs su gtogs pa yin la/'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas su ma gtogs pa gang ci brjod kyang de'ang de dang 'dra ba nyid du gyur/de 'ang de dang 
3a4(1): 'dra ba nyid du 'gyur ro//brjod pa ni dngos po med pa can yang ma yin te/dngos po de 'ang gang zhe na/'phags pa rnams kyi 'phags pa'i shes pa dang /'phags pa'i mthong bas brjod du med pa/
3a5(1): mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa gang yin pa ste/brjod du med pa'i chos nyid de nyid mngon par rdzogs par rtogs par bya ba'i phyir 'dus byas zhes ming du btags so//rigs kyi bu 'dus ma byas zhis bya ba de 'ang ston pas
3a6(1): btags pa'i tshig yin te/ston pas btags pa'i tshig gang yin pa de ni kun tu rtog pa las byung ba tha snyad du brjod pa yin la/kun tu rtog pa las byung pa tha snyad du brjod pa gang yin pa de ni kun du rtog pa sna tshogs kyi tha snyad du brjod pa
3a7(1): gtan yongs su ma grub pa'i phyir 'dus ma byas ma yin no//rigs kyi bu 'dus byas zhes bya ba de 'ang tha snyad kyi khongs su gtogs pa yin la/'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas su ma gtogs pa gang ci brjod kyang de 'ang de dang 'dra ba nyid du
3a8(1): gyur|de 'ang de dang 'dra ba nyidadu 'gyur ro//brjod pa ni dngos po med pa can yang ma yin te/dngos po de 'ang gang zhe na/'phags pa rnams kyis 'phags pa'i shes pa dang /'phags pa'i mthong bas
3a9(1): brjod du med par mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa gang yin pa ste/brjod du med pa'i chos nyid de nyid mngon par rdzogs par rtogs par bya ba'i phyir 'dus ma byas zhes ming du btags so//kye rgyal ba'i sras ji ltar na 'phags pa rnams
3a10(1): kyis dngos po de 'phags pa'i shes pa dang/'phags pa'i mthong bas brjod du med par mngon pa rdzogs par sangs rgyas shing brjod du med pa'i chos nyis de nyid mngon par rdzogs bar rtogs par bya ba'i phyir dus byas dang 'dus ma bya zhes
3b1(1): ming du btags pa yin no//rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/sgyu ma mkhan nam sgyu ma mkhan gyi slob ma mkhas pa zhig lam po che'i bzhi mdor 'dug ste/rtswa dag gam/lo ma dag gam/shing dag gam/gseg
3b2(1): ma dag gam/gyo dum dag gcig tu bsdus nas sgyu ma'i las sna tshogs 'di lta ste/glang po che'i tshogs dang rta pa'i tshogs dang shing rta'i tshogs dang dpung bu chung gi tshogs dang /nor bu dang mu tig dang bai DUr+ya dang dung dang man
3b3(1): shel dang byu ru'i tshogs dang nor dang 'bru dang mdzod dang bang ba'i tshogs rnam par bstan na/de la sems can gang dag byis pa'i rang bzhin can/rmongs pa'i rang bzhin can/shes rab 'chal pa'i rang bzhin can/rtswa
3b4(1): dang lo ma dang shing dang gseg ma dang gyo dum de dag mi shes pa de dag ni de mthong ngam thos na 'di snyam du sems te/glang po che'i tshogs snang ba gang yin pa de ni yod do//rta'i tshogs dang shing rta'i tshog dang dpung bu chung gi chogs
3b5(1): dang nor bu dang mu tig dang pai DUr+ya dang dung dang min shel dang byu ru'i tshogs dag nor dang 'bru dang mdzad dang bang ba'i tshogs snang ba gang yin 'di ni yod do snyam du sems shing /de dag ji ltar mthong ba dang /ji ltar thos pa bzhin du/de
3b6(1): la nan gyis mchog tu bzung zhing mngon par zhen nas/'di ni bden gyi gzhan ni brdzun pa'o zhes rjes su tha snyad 'dogs par yang byed de/de ni de dag gis phyis nye bar brtag par bya dgos pa yin no//de la sems
3b7(1): can gang dag byis pa'i rang bzhin can ma yin pa/rmongs pa'i rang bzhin can ma ma yin pa/shes rab dang ldan pa'i rang bzhin can rtswa dang lo ma dang shing dang gseg ma dang gyo dum de dag shes pa de dag ni de mthong ngam thos na 'di

3b9(1): nor dang 'bru dang mdzod dang bang ba'i tshogs snang ba gang yin 'di ni med kyi/gang la glang po che'i tshogs kyi 'du shes dang /glang po che'i tshogs kyi rnam grangs kyi 'du shes 'byung ba dang /nor dang 'bru dang
3b10(1): mdzod dang bang ba'i tshogs kyi bar gyi 'du shes dang /de dag gi rnam grangs kyi 'du shes byung ba sgyu ma byas pa 'di ni yod/mig slu bar byed pa 'di'i tshad ji snyam du sems shing /de dag ji ltar mthong
4a1(1): ba dang ji ltar thos pa bzhin du de la nan gyis mchog tu bzung zhing mngon par zhen nas 'di ni bden gyi gzhan ni brdzun pa'o zhes rjes su tha snyad mi bdogs kyi/'di ltar don rnam par rig 
4a2(1): par bya ba'i phyir rjes su tha snyad 'dogs par byed de/de ni de dag gis phyis nye bar brtag par bya mi dgos pa yin no//de bzhin du sems can gang dag byis pa'i rang bzhin can so so'i skye bor gyur pa/shes rab

4a4(1): sems te/'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas snang ba gang yin pa 'di ni yod do snyam du sems shing /de dag ji ltar mthong ba dang /ji ltar thos pa bzhin du del nan gyis mchog tu bzungazhing 
4a5(1): mngon par zhen nas 'di na bden gyi gzhan ni brdzus pa'o zhes rjes su tha snyad 'dogs bar yang byed de/de ni de dgag gis phyis nye bar brtags par bya dgos pa yin no//de la sems can gang dag byisapa'i rang bzhin
4a6(1): can ma yin pa bden pa mthong ba/shes rab 'phags pa 'jigs rten las 'das pa thob pa/chos thams can kyi brjod du med pa'i chosanyid mngon par shes pa de dag ni 'dusabyas dang /'dus
4a7(1): ma byas de mthong ngam thos na 'di snyam du sems te/'dus dang byas dang 'dus ma byas dang ba gang yin pa 'di ni med kyi/gang la 'dus byas dang /'dus ma byas kyi 'du shes dang /
4a8(1): 'dus byas dang /'dus pa ma byas kyi rnam grangs kyi 'du shes 'byung ba/rnam par rtog pa las byung ba/'du byed kyi mtshan ma sgyu ma lta bu 'di ni yod/blo rnam par
4a9(1): rmongs par byed pa 'di ni yod do snyam du sems shing de dag ji ltar mthong ba dang /ji ltar thos pa bzhin du de la nan gyis mchog tu bzung zhing mngon par zhen nas 'di ni bden gyi gzhan ni brdzun pa'o zhes rjes su tha snyad
4a10(1): mi 'dogs kyi/'di ltar don rnam par rig par bya ba'i phyir rjes su tha snyad 'dogs par byed de/de ni de dag gis phyis nye bar brtag par bya mi dgos pa yin no//rigs kyi bu de ltar na
4b1(1): 'phags pa rnams kyis dngos po de 'phags pa'i shes pa dang /'phags pa'i mthong bas brjod du med par sangs rgyas shing /brjod du med pa'i chos nyid de nyid mngon par rdzogs par rtogs par 
4b2(1): bya ba'i phyir 'dus byas dang /'dus ma byas zhes ming du brtags pa yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' don zab dgongs pa nges par 'grel gyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//

4b4(1): log par rtogs pa dag/lug dang ba lang dag tu yang skye zhing //de dag shes pa'i smra ba de bor nas//shin tu yun ring 'khor ba 'dir 'khor 'gyur//don zab dgongs pa nges bar 'grel gyi le'u ste dang 
4b5(1): po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' chos 'phags kyis 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das sngon gyi dus rnams kyi shin tu snga ma zhig na/
4b6(1): 'di nas 'jig rten gyi khams gang+gA'i klung bdun cu rtsa bdun gyi bye ma snyed 'das pa na/de bzhin gshegs pa grags pa rgya chen gyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'jig rten gyi khams grags pa can zhes bgyi ba der 
4b7(1): bdag mchis pa'i tshe/de na bdag gis mu stegs can rang rang gi ston pa la sogs pa 'bum phrag bdun cu rtsa bdun 'di lta ste/chos rnams kyi don dam pa'i mtshan nyid bsam pa las brtsams 
4b8(1): nas sa phyogs zhig na mchis shing 'dus nas de dag chos rnams kyi don dam pa'imatshan nyid sems par bgyid/'jal bar bgyid/nye bar rtog par bgyid/yongs su tshol bar bgyid kyang ma 
4b9(1): rtogs nas blo gros tha dad pa yid gnyis can/blo gros ngan pa/rtsod pa dang 'gyed par gyur pa dag gcig la gcig kha stobs kyis gnon pa bgyis/zher 'debs pa bgyis/gab gab bgyis/
4b10(1): mnan pa bgyis/tshar gcad pa bgyis te so sor mchis pa dag gcig mthong nas/bcom ldan 'das bdag 'di snyam bgyid do//kye ma de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten du 'byung zhing de dag

5a2(1): so snyam bgyid lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' chos 'phags la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//chos 'phags de de bzhin no//de 
5a3(1): de bzhin te/ngas ni don dam pa rtog ge thamasacad las yang dag par 'dus pa'i mtshan nyid mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/mngon par rdzogs par sangasargyas nas kyang bsnyed cing gsol 
5a4(1): bar byas/rnam par phye/gdags par byas/rab tu bstan to//de ci'i phyir zhe na/don dam pa ni 'phags pa rnams kyi so so rang gi rig pa yin par ngas bshad la/so so'i 
5a5(1): skye bo rnams kyi phan tshun rig par bya ba ni/rtog ge'i spyod yul yin pa'i phyir te/chos 'phags de'i phyir rnam grangs des khyod kyis 'di ltar rtog ge thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa'i mtshan nyid 
5a6(1): gang yin pa ni don dam pa yin par rig par bya'o//chos 'phags gzhan yang don dam pa ni mtshan ma med pa'i spyod yul yin par ngas bshad la/rtog ge ni mtshan ma'i spyod yul yin te/chos 
5a7(1): 'phags de'i phyir rnam grang des kyang khyod kyis 'di ltar rtog gi thams cad las yang dag bar 'das pa'i mtshan nyid gang yin pa ni don dam ba yin par rig par bya'o//chos 'phags gzhan yang donadam pa ni rtsod pa thams 
5b1(1): cad yang dag par chad pa yin par ngas bshad la/rtog ge ni rtsod pa'i spyod yul yin te/chos 'phags de'i phyir rnam grangs des kyang khyod kyis 'di ltar rtog ge thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa'i mtshan nyid gang 
5b2(1): yin pa ni don dam pa yin par rig par bya'o//chos 'phags 'di lta ste dper na/sgyes bu gang zag ji srid 'tsho ba'i bar du tsha ba dang kha ba'i ro bsten bsten pas ni sbrang rtsi'i ro 'am/sha kha ra'i ro la brtag pa 'am/
5b3(1): rjes su dpag pa'i ma mos par mi nus so//yun ring po nas 'dod pa'i 'dod chags la mos pa/'dod pa'i yongs su gdung ba dag gis yongs su gdungs pa ni nang gi rab tu dben pa'i bde ba gzugs dang sgra
5b4(1): dang dri dang ro dang reg bya'i mtshan ma thams cad dang bral ba la brtag pa 'am/rjes su dpag pa 'am/mos par mi nus so//yul ring po nas kun tu smra ba la mos pa kun tu smra ba la mngon par dga' bas ni nang gi 
5b5(1): mi smra ba 'phags pa'i bde ba la brtag pa'am/rjes su dpag pa 'a ma/mos par mi nus so //yun ring po nas mthong ba dang thos pa dang bye brag phyed pa dang /rnam par shes pa'i tha snyad la mos pa/tha
5b6(1): snyad la mngon par dga' bsani tha snyad thams cad yang dag par chad pa/'jig tshogs la 'gog pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa la brtag pa 'am/rjes du dpag pa 'am/mos pa mi nus so//chos 'phags 'di 
5b7(1): lta ste dper na/yun ring po nas bdag gi yongs su 'dzin pa nyid kyis rtsod pa la brtson pa rtsod pa la mngon par dga' bsi ni byang gi sgra mi snyan pa dag gi bdag gir yongs su 'dzin pa med pa nyid dang /rtsod pa med pa nyid la
5b8(1): brtag pa 'am/rjes su dpag pa 'am/mos par mi nus so//chos 'phagasade bzhin du rtog ge pa thams cad kyis kyang don dam pa rtog ge thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa'i mtshan nyid brtag pa
5b9(1): 'am|rjes su dpag pa 'am/mos par mi nus so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//so so rang rig mtshan nyid spyod yul te/brjod du med cing tha

6b1(1): de de bzhin te/rigs kyi bu de dag thams cad ni 'di ltar don dam pa 'du byed rnams dang tha dad pa dang /tha dad pa ma yin pa nyid las yang dag par 'das pa'i mtshan nyid phra ba mi shes pa/byis pa rmongs pa/mi gsal ba/mi mkhas 
6b2(1): pa/tshul bzhin ma yin par zhugs pa sha stag yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/blo gros shin tu rnam dag 'du byed rnams la de ltar so sor rtog pa dag ni don dam pa rtogs pa 'am/don dam pa mngon sum du byed pa ma yin pa'i phyir 
6b3(1): ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na//blo gros shin tu rnam dag gal te 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid dang /don dam pa'i mtshan nyid tha dad pa ma yin par gyur na ni des na byis pa so so'i skye bo thams cad bden pa mthong ba yin par yang 'gyur/so so'i 
6b4(1): skye bo kho nar gyur bzhin du grub pa dang /bde ba bla na med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa 'thob par yang 'gyur/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar yang 'gyur ro//gal te 'du byed kyi 
6b5(1): mtshan nyid dang /don dmapa'i mtshan nyid tha dad pa yin par gyur na ni/des na bden pa mthong ba rnams kyang 'du byed kyi mtshan ma dang ma bral bar 'gyur/'du byed kyi mtshan ma dang bral ba'i phyir bden pa mthong ba mtshan ma'i 'ching 
6b6(1): ba las rnam par 'grol bar yang mi 'gyur/mtshan ma'i 'ching ba las rnam par ma grol/gnas ngan len gi 'ching ba las kyang rnam par ma grol bar 'gyur/'ching ba de gnyis las ma grol na bden pa mthong bas grub pa dang /
6b7(1): bde ba bla na med pa'i myang ngan las 'das pa thob pa 'ang mi 'gyur/bla na med pa yangadag par rdzogs pa'i byang cub mngon par rdzogs par tshang rgya bar yang mi 'gyur ro//blo gros shin tu rnam dag gang gi phyir so so'i skye bo bden pa mthong 
6b8(1): ba ma yin/so so'i skye bo kho nar gyur bzhin du grub pa dang /bde ba bla na med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa 'thob par yang mi 'gyur/bla na med par yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar mi 'gyur ba de'i phyir 'du 
6b9(1): byed kyi mtshan nyid dang /don dam pa'i mtshan nyid tha dad pa ma yin zhes byar mi rung ste/de la gang dag de skad ces 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid dang /don dam pa'i mtshan nyid thad pa ma yin zhes zer ba de dag ni rnam grangs des na khyod kyis 'di ltar 
6b10(1): tshul bzhin ma yin par zhugs pa yin gyi/tshul bzhin du zhugs pa ma yin par rig par bya'o//blo gros shin tu rnam pa gang gi phyir bden pa mthong ba rnams 'du byed kyi mtshan ma dang bral ba ma yin gyi/bral ba kho na yin pa dang /bden pa 
7a1(1): mthong ba mtshan ma'i 'ching ba las rnam par grol ba ma yin gyi/rnam par grol ba yin pa dang /bden pa mthong ba gnas ngan len gyi 'ching ba las rnam par gol ba ma yin gyi/rnam pa 
7a2(1): grol ba dang /'ching ba de gnyis ka las rnam par grol na grub pa dang /bde ba bla na med pa'i phyir mya ngan las 'das pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dang /bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 
7a3(1): yang 'gyur ba de'i phyir 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid dang /don dam pa'i mtshan nyid tha dad pa zhes byar mi rung ste/de la gang dag de skad ces 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid dag/don dam pa'i mtshan nyid pa tha dad pa zhes zer ba de dag ni rnam grangs 
7a4(1): des na khyod kyis 'di ltar tshul bzhin ma yin par zhugs pa yin gyi/tshul bzhin du zhugs pa ma yin par rig par bya'o//blo gros shin tu rnam dag gzhan yang gal te 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid da /don dam pa'i mtshan 
7a5(1): nyid tha dad pa ma yin par gyur na ni/des na ji ltar 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid kun nas nyon mongs pa'i mtshan nyid du gtogs pa de bzhin du don dam pa'i mtshan nyid kyang kun nas nyon mongs pa'i 'tshan nyid du gtogs par 'gyur ro//blo gros shin tu 
7a6(1): rnam dag gal te 'du byed kyi mtshananyid dang /don dam pa'i 'tshan nyid tha dad pa yin par gyur na'ang /des na 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid thams cad la don dam pa'i mtshan nyidaspyi'i mtshan nyid du gyur pa ma yin par 'gyur ro//blo gros 
7a7(1): shin tu rnam dag gang gi phyir don dam pa'i mtshan nyid kun nas nyon mngos pa'i mtshan nyid du gtos pa ma yin pa dang /'du byed kyi mtshan nyid tham cad la don dam pa'i mtshanid spyi'i mtshan nyid du gyur ba yin pa de'i phyir 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid
7a8(1): don dam pa'i mtshan nyid tha dad pa ma yin zhes byar yang mi rung la/don dam pa'i mtshan nyid ba dad pa zhes byar yang mi rung ste/de la gang dag de skad ces 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid dang /don dam pa'i mtshan nying tha dad pa ma yin pa zhes zer 
7a9(1): ba dang /don dam pa'i mtshon nyid tha dad pa zhes zer ba de dag ni/rnam grangs des na khyod kyis 'di ltar tshul bzhin ma yin par zhugs pa yin gyi/tshul bzhin du zhugs pa ma yin par rig par bya'o//blo gros shin tu rnam 
7a10(1): dag gzhan yang gal te 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid dag/don dam pa'i mtshan nyi tha dad pa ma yin par gyur na ni/des na ji ltar don dam pa'i mtshan nyid 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid tha mas cad la bye brag med pa de bzhin du 'du byed kyi mtshan
7b1(1): nyid thams cad kyang bye brag med pa dang /rnal 'byor pa dag 'du byed rnams la ji ltar mthong ba dang ji ltar thos pa dang ji ltar bye brag phyed pa dang ji ltar rnam par shes pa las gong du don dam pa yongs su 'tshol bar yang mi 'gyur ro//gal te
7b2(1): 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid dang /don dam pa'i mtshan nyid tha dad pa yin par gyur na ni/des na 'du byed rnams kyi bdag med pa tsam dang /ngo bo nyid med pa tsam nyid don dam pa'i mtshan nyid yin par yang mi 'gyur/kun nas nyon mongs pa'i mtshan
7b3(1): nyid dang /rnam par byang ba'i mtshan nyid kyang dus gcig tu mtshan nyid tha dad du grub par 'gyur ro//blo gros shin tu rnam dag gang gi phyir 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid rnams ni bye brag yod pa yin te/bye brag med pa ma yin pa dang /rnal 'byor pa bag 'du
7b4(1): byed rnams la ji ltar mthonang ba dang /thos pa dang /bye brag phyed pa dang /rnam par shes pa las gong la don dam pa yongs su tshol bar byed pa dang /don dam pa ni 'du byed rnams kyi bdag yed pas rab tu phye ba yin pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /
7b5(1): rnam par byang ba'i mtshan nyis kyang dus gcig tu mtshan nyad tha dad du grub pa med pa de'i phyir 'du byed kyi mtshan nyad dang /don dam pa'i mtshan nyid tha dad pa ma yin pa 'am/tha dad pa zhes byar mi rung ngo//de la gang dag de skad ces 'du byed kyi mtshan
7b6(1): nyid dang /don dam pa'i mdzan nyid tha dad pa ma yinapa 'am/tha dad pa zhes zer ba de dag ni rnam grangs des na/khyod kyis 'di ltar tshul bzhin ma yin par zhugs pa yin gyi/tshul bzhin du zhugs pa ma yin par rig par bya'o//blo gros
7b7(1): shin tu rnam dag 'di lta ste dper na/dung gi dkar po nyid ni/dung dang mtshan nyid tha dad pa ma ma yin pa 'am/mtshan nyid tha dad du gdags par slab ma yin no//dung gi dkar po nyid ji lta ba bzhin du gser gyi ser po nyid kyang de bzhin no//
7b8(1): pi bang gi sgra'i snyan pa nyid kyang pi wang gi sgra dang mtshan nyid tha dad ma yin pa 'am/mtshan nyad tha dad par gdags par sla ba ma yin no//a ka ru nag po'i dri zhim pa nyid kyang a ka ru nag po dang mtshan nyid tha dad la ma yin pa dang mtshan nyid
7b9(1): tha dad du gdags par sla ba ma yinano//na le sham gyi tsha ba nyid kyang na le sham dang mtshan nyid tha dad sa ma yin pa 'am/mtshan nyed tha dad du gdags par slob ma yin no//na le sham gyi tsha banyid ji lta ba bzhin a ru ra'i bska ba nyid kyang
7b10(1): de bzhin no//'di lta ste dper na/shing bal gyi 'da' ba'i 'jam pa nyid kyang shing bal gyi 'da' ba dang mtshan nyid tha dad pa ma yin pa 'am/mtshan nyid tha dad bu gdags par sla ba ma yi no//'di lta ste dper ni/mar la mar gyi snying khu'ang mar
8a1(1): @#//dang mtshan nyid tha dad pa ma yin pa 'am/mtshan nyid tha dad du gdags par sla ba ma yin no//'di lta ste dper na/'du byed thams cad la mi rtag pa nyid dang /zag pa dang bcas pa thams cad
8a2(1): la sdug bsngal ba nyid dang /chos thams cad la gang bdag med pa nyid kyang de dag dang mtshan nyid tha dad la ma yin pa 'am/mtshan nying tha dad du gdags par slo ba ma yin no//blo gros shin tu rnam dag 'di lta ste dper na 'dod chags kyi
8a3(1): ma zhi ba'i mtshan nyid dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa'i mtshan nyid kyang 'dod chags dang mtshan nyid tha dad par ma yin pa 'am/mtshan nyad tha dad du gdags par smra ba ma yin pa dang /'dod chags kyi ji lta ba bzhin du zhe sdang dang gti mug gi'ang
8a4(1): de bzhin du rig par bya'o//blo gros shin tu rnam gang de bzhin du 'du byed kyi mtshan nyid dang /don dam pa'i mtshan nyid kyang mtshan nyid tha dad pa ma yin pa 'am/mtshan nyed tha dad du gdags par mi bzod do//blo gros shin tu rnam pa dag ngas ni de
8a5(1): ltar don dam pa phra ba mchog tu phra ba/mchog tu zab pa/rtogs par dka' ba/mchog tu dka' ba/tha dad pa dang /tha dad pa ma yin pa nyid las yang dag par 'das pa'i mtshan nyid mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/mngon par rdzogs par sad
8a6(1): rgyas nas kyang bsnyad cing gsal bar byas/rnam par phye/gdags pa byas/rab tu bstan to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//'du byed khams dang don dam mtshan nyid ni//gcig dang tha dad bral
8a7(1): ba'i mtshan nyid de//gcig dang tha dad du yang gang rtog pa//de dag tshul bzhin ma yin zhugs pa yin//skye ba po yi lhag mthong dang //zhi gnas goms par byas nas ni//gnas ngan len gyi 'ching ba dang //mtshan ma'i 'ching las rnam
8a8(1): grol 'gyur//blo gros shin tu rnam dag gi le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das kis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor khyod kyis sems can gyi khams na gang dag
8a9(1): mngon pa'i nga rgyal gyis mngon du zin cing shes pa brda sprod par byed pa'i sems can ni ji tsam zhig yod par shes/khyod kyis sems can gyi khyams na gang dag mngon pa'i nga rgyal med par shes pa brda sprod par byedapa'i sems ni ji tsam
8a10(1): zhig yod par shes/rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das bdag gi sems can gyi khams na gag dag mngon pa'i nga rgyal ma mchis par shes pa brda sprod pa bgyid pa'i sems can ni nyung shes shig mchis par 'tshal
8b1(1): lags kyi/bcom ldan 'das bdag gis sems can gyi khams na gang dag mngon pa'i nga rgyal gyis mngon du zin cing shes pa brda sprod par bgyid pa'i sems can ni tshad ma mchis grangs ma mchis/rjod du ma mchis par
8b2(1): 'tshal lags te/bcom ldan 'das bdagadus gcig cig na/dgon pa nags khrod chen po zhig na mchis pa'i tshe/de na bdag gi nye 'khor na dge slong rab tu mang po dag gcig kyang dgon pa nags khrod chen pon
8b3(1): rten cing gnas lags te/bdag gis snga dro'i dus kyi tshe dge slong phan tshun nas mchis shing mjal nas/chos rnam pa sna tshogs dmigs pa'i mngon par rtogs pa ston pas shes pa brda sprod par bgyed
8b4(1): de/de na kha cig ni phung po dmigs pa dang /phung po'i mtshan ma dmigs pa dang /phung bo'i skye ba dmigs pa dang /phungapo'i 'jigs pa dmigs pa dang /phungabo 'gog pa dmigs pa dang /
8b5(1): phung bo 'gog pa mngon du bgyid pa dmigs pas shes pa brda sprod pa bgyid/kha cig phung po dmigs pas ji lta ba de bzhin du kha cig ni skye mched dmigs pas/kha cig rten cing 'brel bar
8b6(1): 'byung ba dmigs pas shes pa bar+d+ra sprod par bgyid kha cig ni zas dmigs pa dang /zas kyi mtshan ma dmigs pa dang/zas kyi skye ba dmigs pa dang/
8b7(1): zas kyi 'jig pa dmigs pa dang /zas 'gog pa dmigs pa dang/zas 'gog pa mngon du bgyi ba dmigs pas shes pa brnga sprod par bgyid/
8b8(1): kha cig ni bden pa dmigs pa dang /bden pa'i mtshan nyid dmigs pa dang /bden pa yongs su shes pa dmigs pa dang /bdenapa spangs pa dmigs pa dang //bden pa mngon du bgyi
9a1(1): //pa dmyigs pa dang/bden pa sgom pa dmyigs bas /dmyigs pas shes pa rda srod par bgyid/kha cig ni khams dmyigs pa dang/khams kyi mtshan ma dmyigs pa dang/khams gyi sna tshogs nyid dmyigs pa dang/khams du ma
9a2(1): pa dmyigs pa dang/khams 'gog pa dmyigs pa dang/khamasa'agog pa mngon gsum du bgyi ba dmyigs pas/shes pa rda srod par bgying//kha cig ni dran pa nye bar bzhag pa dmyigs pa dang/dran pa nye bar bzhag ba'i myi
9a3(1): thun ba'i phyogs dang/gnyen po dmyigs pa dang/dran pa nye bar bzhag pa sgom pa dmyigs pa dang/dran panye bar bzhag pa ma skyes pa skye ba dmyigs pa dang/dran ba nye bar bzhag pa skye ba gnas pa dang/myi sgyud pa dang/slar zhing 'byung pa dang
9a4(1): 'phel zhing rgyas pa dmyigs pa dang/shes pa rda srod par gyid/kha cig ni dran ba nye bar bzhag ba ci lta ba bzhin du/kha cig ni yang dag par spong pa rnams dang/rdzu 'phrul gi rkang pa rnams dang/dbang po rnams dang/stobs rnams dang/
9a5(1): byang chub kyi yan lag rnams dang/'phags pa'i lam yan lag pa rgyad pa'i chos nyid dmyigs pa dang/'phags pa'i lam lan lag brgyad pa'i mtshan nyid dmyigs pa dang/'phaDa pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i
9a6(1): mthun ba'i phyogs kyi gnyen po dmyigs pa/'phags pa'i lam yan pa rgyad ba'i sgom pa dmyigs pa dang /'phags pa'i lam yan la brgyad pa skyes pa skye ba dmyigs ba dang/'phags pa'i lam yin lag brgyad
9a7(1): pa skyes gas gnas pa dang/myi skyung ba dang/slar zhing 'byung ba dang/'phel zhing rgyas pa dang/shes pa rda sprod pa mthong ba lags so//mthong nas kyang bdag 'di snyam du bgyid de/tshe dang ldan pa 'di dag ni/chos
9a8(1): rnam pa sna tshogs dmyigs pa'i mngon par rtogs ba/stond par shes ba/rda srod par bgyid pa/'di ltar don dam ba thams cad du/ro cig pa'i mtshan nyid ma 'tshal bas/de tshe dang ldan pa de dag thams cad ni mngon pa'i nga rgyal can
9a9(1): mngon pa'i nga rgyal gis/mngon tu gzird cing shes pa rda srod pa sha stag go//snyam bgyid lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis gang gi slad du/'di skad don dam pa ni phra ba/zab pa/rtogs par dka' ba/thams cad du
9a10(1): ro cig pa'i mtshan nyid yin no/zhes bka' ma lad pa//ci tsam du bcom ldan 'das kyis legs par gsungs pa de ni/ngo tshar lags te/bcom ldan 'das don dam pa thams cad du ro cig pa'i mtshan nyid ni/bcom ldan 'das

9b6(1): lag rnams dang/rab 'byord 'phags pa'i lam yan lagabargyad pa la rnam dag pa'i dmyigs pa dang/gang dag yin pa de ni/ngas don/dam pa yin pa'i yongs su bstan pa'i phyir ro//phung po rnams

9b8(1): pa yin par rig par bya'o//rab 'byord gzhan yang dge slong rnal 'byord pa'i phung po rnams dang/khams rnams dang/skye mched rnams dang/rtend cing 'brel bar 'byung ba dang/zas rnams dang/bden pa rnams dang/

10a1(1): /rnam par 'dzind pa dang/mngon par rdzogs par kho na byed de/rab 'byord rnam grangs des na/khyod kyis 'di ltar thams cad da ro cig pa'i mtshan nyid gang yin pa'i ni/don dam pa yin par rig par bya'o/
10a2(1): ka rab 'byord gzhan yang ci ltar phung po dang/skye mched dang/rtend cing 'brel par 'byung ba dang/zas dang/bden pa dang/khams dang/dran pa nye bar bzhag pa rnams dang/yang dag par spong pa rnams dang/rdzu 'phrul

10a4(1): bzhin du/gal te chos de dag ni/de bzhin nyid don dam pa chos bdag myed pa yang mtshan nyid/tha dad pa yin par gyurd na ni/des na de bzhin nyid don dam pa/chos bdag myed pa yang rgyu dang bcas pa yin zhing /
10a5(1): rgyu las byung ba yin par 'gyur ro//rab 'byord gang gis phyir/rgyu las 'byung ba nyid ma yin na ni/'du byas yin bar 'gyur ro//'dus byas nyid yin na ni/don dam pa ma yin par 'gyur ro//
10a6(1): don dam par ma yin na ni/don dam ba gzhan zhig yongs su btsal dgos par 'gyur ro//rab 'byord gang gi phyir/de bzhin nyid don dam ba chos bdag myed pa/rgyu las byung pa ma yin pa dang/'dus byas ma
10a7(1): yin pa dang/don dam pa ma yin ba dang/don dam pa de'i don dam pa gzhan yongs su btsal bar bya myi dgos kyi/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams byung yang rung/ma byung yang rung ste/rtag pa rtag pa'i dus dang/ther zug ther zu
10a8(1): ga gi dus su/chos gnas par bya ba'i phyir/chos rnams kyi chos nyid kyi dbyings de ni/rnam par gnas pa kho na yin pa de'i phyir ro//rab 'byord rnam grangs des kyang/khyod kyis ci ltar thams cad du ro cig pa'i mtshan nyid
10a9(1): gang yin pa de ni/don dam pa yin par rig par bya'o//rab 'byord 'di lta ste dper na/nam mkha'i khams kyi rnam pa sna tshogs du/mtshan nyid du tha dad pa la mtshan ma myed par gyurd ba ste/thams cad ro cig pa'i
10a10(1): mtshan nyid yin te/rab 'byord de bzhin du don dam par yang/mtshan nyid tha dad pa'i chos rnams la thams cad du/ro cig pa'i mtshan nyid yin par ro lta bar bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe/tshigs su gcad pa 
10b1(1): 'di dag bka' stsald to//don dam pa de ni tha dad pa yin te/kun tu ro cig de ni sangs rgyas gsung/de la tha dad gang dag kun rtand pa/de dag nga rgyal gnas pa rmongs pa yin///
10b2(1): //de nas bcom ldan 'das la/byang chub sems dpa' blo gros yangs pas zhus pa/bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' dang/yid dang rnam par
10b3(1): shes pa'i gsang ba la mkhas pa'i zhes bgyi' na/bcom ldan 'das ci tsam gis na byang chub sems dpa' sem yid dang/rnam par shes pa'i gsang ba la mkhasapa lags/de bzhin gshegs pa byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' dang
10b4(1): yid dang rnam par shes pa'i gsang pa la mkhas par dogs na yang/ci tsam gis dogs lags/de skad ces gsold pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis/byang chub sems dpa'  blo gros yangs pa la/'di skad ces bka' stsald to//
10b5(1): blo gros yangs pa khyod de ltar skye po mang po la sman pa dang/skye po mang po la bde' pa dang/'jig rtend la snying rtse ba dang/lha dang myir bcas pa'i skyes dgu'i don dang phan pa dang/bde' pa'i phyir bzhugs te/de bzhin gshegs
10b6(1): pa la khyod kho na nyid zhu bar sems dpa' na/legs so legs so/blo gros yangs pa de'i phyir nyon cig dang/sems dang/yid dang/rnam par/shes pa'i gsang ba la chags pa khyod la bshad par bya'o//blo gros
10b7(1): yangs pa/'gro ba drug gi 'khor ba 'di na/sems can gang dag sems can gi gang dang gang tu/yang na sgo nga las skyes pa'i skye gnas dang/yang na mngal las skyes pa'i skye gnas sam/yang na drod sher las bskyes pa 'am/
10b8(1): yang na rdzus te skyes pa'i skyes gnas su lus mngon par grub cing/'byung bar 'gyur ba der/dang po de ltar len pa rnam pa gnyis po rtend dang/bcas pa'i dbang po gzugs can lend ba dang/mtshan ma dang/mying dang rnam par rtog pa
10b9(1): la tha snyad du 'dogs pa'i/spros pa'i bag chags len pa la rtend nas/sa bon thams cad sems rnam par smind cing 'jug la/rgyas shing rnam par 'phel ba dang/rgyas par 'gyur ro//de la gzugs can gi khams ni
10b10(1): lend pa gnyis yod la/gzugs can ma yin pa'i khams na ni/lend pa gnyis su myed do//blo gros yangs pa rnam par shes pa de ni/lend pa'i rnam par shes pa de ni/lend pa'i rnam par she ba zhes kyang bya ste
11a1(1): //'di ltar des lus 'di la gzung zhing blang ba'i phyir ro//kun bzhi'i rnam par shes pa zhes bya ste/'di ste/des lus 'di la grub pa dang/bde ba cig pa'i don kyis kun tu sbyor pa dang/rab tu sbyor ba myed pa'i
11a2(1): phyir ro//sems zhes kyang bya ste/'di ltar de ni gzugs dang/sgra dang/dri dang/ro dang/reg bya dang/chos rnams kun tu bas chags pa dang/nye bar bcags pa pa'i phyir ro//blo gros yangs pa nye bar len pa'i rnam par shes
11a3(1): pa de la rten cing gnas nas/rnam par shes pa'i tshogs drug po 'di lta ste/myig gi rnam par shes pa dang/rna ba dang/sna dang/lce dang/lus dang/yid kyi rnam par shes pa dag 'byung ngo//des na rnam par shes pa dang/
11a4(1): bcas pa'i myig dang/gzugs rnams la rtend nas/myig gi rnam par shes par phyung ste/myig gi rnam par shes de dang/lhan cig du rjes su 'jug ba dus mtshungs pa/spyod yul mtshungs pa/rnam par rtog pa yid kyi
11a5(1): rnam par shes pa yang 'byung ngo//blo gros yangs pa de la rnam par shes pa dang bcas pa'i rna ba dang/sna dang/lce dang lus dang yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang/btsas pa'i lus dang/reg bya rnams
11a6(1): la rtend nas/lus kyi rnam par shes pa 'byung ste/lus kyi rnam par shes pa de dang/lhan cig tu rjes su 'jug pa/dus mtshungs pa/spyod yul bar rtog pa'i yid kyi rnam par shes pa
11a7(1): 'byung ngo//gal te myig gi rnam par shes pa cig/lan cig byung na/myig gi rnam par shes pa dang/spyod yul mtshungs pa/rnams par rtog pa'i yid kyi rnam pa shes pa yang cig kho na /lan cig 'byung ngo//blo gros yang
11a8(1): pa 'di ste ste/dper na chu klung chen po 'bab pa la/gal te rlabs cig byung ba'i rkyend nye bar gnas par gyurd na/rlabs kyang cig kho na 'byung ngo//gal te rlabs gnyis sam/gal te rab tu mang po 'byung ba'i rkyend nye bar
11a9(1): gnas par gyurd na/rlabs rab tu mang po dag 'byung zhing /chu klung de rang gi rgyud kyis rgyun chags par yang myi 'gyur la/yongs su zad par yang myi 'gyur ro//me long gi dkyil 'khor shin tu yongs su dag pa 'byung ba la yang/gal te
11a10(1): gzugs brnyan cig byung ba'i rkyen nye bar gnas par gyur na/gzugs rnyan yang cig kho na 'byung ngo/gal te gzugs rnyan gnyis sam/gal te rab du mang po dag 'byung ba'i rkyend nye par gnas par gyurd na/bzugs rnyan rab tu
11b1(1): mang po dag 'byung zhing me long gi dkyil 'khor de /gzugs rnyand gi dngos por yongs su 'gyur ba yang ma yin la/yongs su sbyor par yang myi mngon no //blo gros yangs pa de bzhin du /chu klung lta bu dang/me long lta bu'i 
11b2(1): len pa'i rnam par shes pa de la/rtend cing gnas nas /gal te myig gi rnam par shes pa cig/lan cig 'byung ba'i rkyend nye bar gnas par gyurd na/yang myig gi rnam par shes pa gcig kho na lan cig 'byung ngo//gal te rnam
11b3(1): par shes pa'i tshogs lnga car bgyi ba dag /lan cig 'byung pa'i rkyend nye bar gnas bar gyurd na yang/rnam par shes pa'i tshogs /lnga car lan cig 'byung ngo//blo gros yangs ba /de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i chos 
11b4(1): kyi lus shes bya ba la rten cing/chos kyi lus shes bya ba la gnas nas /sems dang rnam par shes pa'i gsang ba la chags pa yin/yang de bzhin gshegs pa ni/byang chub sems dpa' sems dang/yid dang/rnam
11b5(1): par shes pa'i gsang ba la mkhas par dogs na/de tsam gis thams cad nas thams cad du myi dogs so//blo gros yangs pa gang gi phyir /byang chub sems dpa' nang gi so so /nang gi len pa 
11b6(1): myi mthong/len pa'i rnam par shes pa yang myi mthong la/de yang yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhin du yin pa dang/kun zhi yang mthong/kun zhi'i rnam par shes par yang myi mthong/bsags pa mthong /sems
11b7(1): kyang myi mthong/myig kyang myi mthong/gzugs kyang myi mthong/myig gi rnam par shes pa myi mthong/rna pa yang myi mthong/sgra yang myi mthong/rna pa'i rnam par shes pa yangamyi mthong/sna yang myi mthong/dri yang myi mthong 
11b8(1): sna'i rnam par shes pa yang mi mthong/lce yang myi mthong/ro yang myi mthong/lce'i rnam par shes pa yang mye mthong/lus kyang myi mthong/reg kyang myi mthong/lus kyi rnam par shes pa yang myi 'thong zhing //blo gros yangs pa
11b9(1): gang gi phyir byang chub sems dpa' nang ni so so/rang gi yid kyang myi mthong /chos kyang myi mthong/yid kyi rnam pa shes pa yang myi mthong/de yang yang dag par ci lta ba bzhin du yin/de ni byang chub sems dpa' 'dod pa 
11b10(1): mkhas pa zhes bya'o//de bzhin gshegs pa yang byang chub sems dpa' don dam pa la mkhas pa ni/sems dang/yid dang/rnam par shes pa'i gsang ba la mkhas par dogs na/yang de tsam gis dogs so//
12a1(1): //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe/tshigs su gcad pa 'di dag bka' stsald to//len pa'i rnam par shes pa zab cing 'phra/sa bon thams cad chu bo'i rgyun ltar 'bab /bdag tu rtogs par gyurd na
12a2(1): /myi rur shes/byis pa rnam langs de ma stand////blo gros yangs pa'i le'u ste lnga ba'o////
12a3(1): 'phags pa dgongs pa nye bar 'greld pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo'//bam po gnyis pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'da'as la byang chub sems
12a4(1): dpa' yon tan 'byung gnas kyis zhu ba zhus pa/bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid la mkhas pa/byang chub sems dpa' chos pa rnams
12a5(1): kyi mtshan nyid la mkhas pa zhes bya ba ni/bcom ldan 'das ci tsam gis na byang chub sems dpa' chosarnams kyi mtshan nyid la mkha' ba la gas/de bzhin gshegs pa byang chub sems dpa' chosakyi rnam
12a6(1): grangs mtshan nyid la mkhas par dogs na yang/ci tsam gis dogs lags/de skad ces gsol ba dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' yon tan 'byung nas la/'di skad ces gso ba to//
12a7(1): yon tan 'byung nas khyod de ltar skye po mang po la phan pa dang/bde' ba dang/'jig rtend la snying rtse ba dang/lha dang myir bcas pa'i skye dgu'i don dang/phan pa dang/bde' pa'i phyir bzhugs te/de bzhin gshegs pa la khyod don 'di
12a8(1): nyid dri par sems dpa' ni/legs so legs so/yon tan 'byung nas de'i phyir nyon cig/bsod nams kyi mtshan nyid la mkhas ba/khyod la bshad par bya'o//yon tan 'byung nas chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid ni 'phung po
12a9(1): 'di dag yin te/gsum gang zhe na/kun rtags pa'i mtshan nyid dang/gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nyid dang/yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid ni/yon tan 'byung nas de la chos rnams kyi gakun rtags pa'i mtshan nyidagang zhe na/ci tsam gis
12a10(1): rjes su tha snyad btags pa'i phyir/chos rnams kyi ngo bo nyid dam/bye brag tu mying dang dar rnam par bzhag pa gang yin pa'o//yon tan 'byung nas tshos rnams kyi gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nying gang zhe na/chos rnams kyi rtend cing 'brel bar
12b1(1): 'byung ba nyid do//'di lta ste /'di yod pas 'di 'byung la/myi skye pa'i phyir 'di skyes pa/'di lta ste/ma rig pa'i rkyend kyis 'du byed/rnam par shes pa byas nas/de ltar na sdug sngal gi phung po chen po 'ba' shig 'byung bar bya'o//
12b2(1): zhes bya ba'i bar gang yin pa'o//yon 'byung nas /chos kyi yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid gang zhe na/chos rnams kyi de bzhin nyid gang yin pa de/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi brtund pa'i rgyu dang/legs par tshul bzhin yid byas pa'i
12b3(1): rgyu de rtogs shing/de rtogs pa de goms par byas ba 'am/yang dag grub pas kyang /bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par yang dag par grub pa gang yin pa'o//yon tan 'byung nas 'di lta ste dper na/skyes bu
12b4(1): gang zag rab 'bring can gi skyon chags pa de lta bur ni/kun rtags pa'i mtshan nyid la bar bya'o//yon tan 'byung nas 'di lta ste/dper na/de bzhin nyid la rab 'bring gi mtshan ma skra bshad 'dzings pa 'am/sbrang ma 'am
12b5(1): til gi 'bru 'am/sngon po'i mtshan ma 'am/ser po'i mtshan ma 'am/dmar po'i mtshan ma 'am/dkar po'i mtshan ma 'am/snang bar gyurd pa de lta bas na/gzhan gi dbang mtshan nyid lta bar bya'o//
12b6(1): yon tan 'byung nas 'di lta ste dper na/skyes pu gang zag de nyid kyi myig yo ngas su dag cing/myig la rab rib kyi skyon chags pa dang/pra pa ra gyurd pa na/myig de nyid kyi rang bzhin gi spyod yul /ma nord
12b7(1): ba'i spyod yul de lta bu yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid lta bar bya'o//yon tan 'byung nas 'di lha ste dper na/shel shind tu gsal ba'i gang gi tshe/sngon po dang phrad bar gyurd pa de'i tshe ni/nor bu rin po che an da snyil
12b8(1): dang /thon ka chen po lta bur snang bar gyurd cing/nor bu rin po che and snyil dang/thon ka chen por log par 'dzind pas kyang /sems rnam par rmongs par byed de/gang gi tshe mtshon dmar po dang/phrad par gyurd pa de'i tshe/nor bu
12b9(1): nor bu rin po che bang ma ra lang ba (cad)lta bur snang par 'gyur zhing/nor bu rin po che pad ma ra lang pa lta bur (log par)'dzind kyang/sems can rnam par rmongs par byed/gang gi tshe mtshon ljang ku dang/phrad par gyurd ba de'i tshe/nor bu 
12b10(1): rin po che dmar rgan lta bur snang bar 'gyur/nor bu rin po che dmar rgan lta bur log par 'dzind pas kyang sems can rnams rnam par rmongs bar byed do//gang gi tshe mtshon ser po dang/phrad par gyur pa de'i tshe ni/ser po
13a1(1): //lta bur snang bar 'gyur zhing /gser du log par 'dzind pas kyang /sems can rnams rnam par rmongs par byed do//yon tan 'byung nas/'di lta ste dper na/shel shel shind du dkar ba mtshon
13a2(1): ka dang phrad pa de lta bu ni gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nyid kyi tha snyad kyi bag chags lta bar bya'o//'di lta ste dper na/shel shol shind tu gsal ba la/nor bu rin po che an da snyil dang/thon ka chen po dang/pad ma dang
13a3(1): ma rgad dang/gser du log par 'dzin pa dang/de lta bur ni gzhan gi dbang gis mtshan nyid la/kun rtags pa'i mtshan nyid du 'dzind pa lta bar bya'o//yon tan 'byung nas 'di lta ste dper na/shel shind du gsal ba de nyid
13a4(1): de lta bur ni/gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nyad lta bar bya'o//'di lta sta dber na/shel shind tu gsal ba de nyid nor bu rin po che an dang snyil dang/mthong ka chen po dang/pad mar lad dang/mar rgad dang/gser gi mtshan nyid rtag pa
13a5(1): rtag pa'i dus dang/ther zug ther zug gi dus yongs su ma grub cing ngo bo nyid myed pa de lta bur ni gzhan gi dpang gi mtshan nyid de/kun tu rtags pa'i mtshan nyid re rtag pa rtag pa'i dus dang/ther zasug
13a6(1): ther zug gi dus su yongs su ma grub cing/ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid kyis yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid lta bar bya'o//yon tan byung nas de la mtshan ma dang/'breld pa'i mying la rtend nas ni//
13a7(1): skyon rtags pa'i mtshan nyid rab tu shes so//gzhan gi dpang mtshan nyid la kun rtags pa'i mtshan nyid du/mngon par zhen pa la rtend nas ni/gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nyid du rab tu shes so//gzhan gyi dbang gi mtsha
13a8(1): na nyid la/kun tu rtags pa'i mtshananyid la/mngon par zhend pa myed pa la rtend nas ni/yongs su grub pa'i mtshananyid du shes so//yon tan 'byung nas de la byang chub sems dpa' chos rnams kyis/gzhan gi dbang
13a9(1): gi mtshan nyid la/kun tu rtagasapa'i mtshan nyid yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes so//mtshan nyid myed pa'i chos ci lta ba bzhin du rab du shes so//yon tan byung nas del byang chub sems dpa' gzhan gi
13a10(1): dbang gis/mtshan nyid yang dag pa cilta ba bzhin du rab tu shesaso//kun nas nyon mongs pa'i mtshan nyid kyi/chos yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhi du rab tu shes so//yon tan 'byud nas de la/byang chub sems dpa'
13b1(1): yongs su grub pa'i dbang gi mtshan nyid /yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes so//rnam par byang ba'i mtshan nyid kyi chos /yang dag pa ci lta pa bzhin du rab tu shes so //yon tan ('byung nas)de la byang chub sems dpa'
13b2(1): gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nyid myed pa'i chos /yang dag pa ci ltar ba bzhin du rab tu shes na/kun nas nyon mongs pa'i mtshan nyid kyi chos rab tu spong pa'i/kun nas nyon mongs pa 'i mtshan nyid kyi chos spangs nas /rnam par byang ba'i
13b3(1): mtshan nyid ki chos thob par gyurd te/yon tan 'byung nas gang gi phyir/byang chub sems dpas de ltar chos rnams kyi kun nas nyon mongs pa'i mdzan nyid dang/gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nying dang/yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid 
13b4(1): yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhinadu rab tu shes so//shing mtshan nyid myed pa dang/kun nas nyon mongs pa'imatshan nyid dang/rnam par byang ba'i mtshan nying dang/yang dag pa ci lta ba bzhin du rab tu shes la/mtshan nyid myed pa'i chos
13b5(1): yang dag pa ci lta ba ba zhin du rab tu shes so//kun nas nyon mongs pa'i mtshin nyid kyi chos rab tu spor zhing/kun nas nyon mongs ba'i mtshan nyid kyi chos rab du spangs nas/rnam par byang ba'i chos gnyis kyi chos
13b6(1): rab tu spang pa de tsam gis na byang chub sems dpas/chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid kyi la mkhas pa yang yin la/de bzhin gshegs pa byang chub sems dpa'i mtshan nyid kyi chos rab tu sbangs nas/chos rnams
13b7(1): kyi mtshan nyid la mkhas pa/dogs na yang de tsam gyis dogs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe/tshigs su gcad pa de dag bka' sacalang to//mtshan nyid myed pa'i chos ni rab shes dang//kun nas nyon
13b8(1): mongs mtshan nyid chos spong 'gyur//kun nas nyon mongs chos ni rab spangs nas//shind tu rnam dag mtshan nyid chos thob 'gyur//skye bo nyes tsom le lo can//'du byed skyon la rtog par myi byed pa//gtan yod
13b9(1): g.yo' pa yod pa'i chos rnams la//rab tu nyams par snying rjer bya ba yin//yon tan 'byung nas kyi le'u ste drug pa'o////
13b10(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' don dam yang dag 'phags la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di na bdag cig bu dben pa zhig na 'dug pa'i tshe//sems kyi yongs su rtogs pa 'di lta bu skyes
14a1(1): /7/lags te/bcom ldan 'das kyis rnam grangs du mar/phung po rnams kyi rang gi mtshan nyid kyang bka' sa lang/skye ba'i mtshan nyid dang/'jig pa'i mtshan nyid dang/spang ba dang/yongs su shes pa yang bka'
14a2(1): sa lang/phung po rnams kyi lta ba de bzhin du/skye mched rnams dang/rtend cing 'brel bar 'byung bar 'gyurd pa dang/kha zas kyi yang bka' sa lang/bcom ldan 'das kyis rna grangs du mar/bden pa rnams kyi rang gi mtshan nyi
14a3(1): kyang bka' sa lang/yongs su shes pa dang/spang ba dang/mngon tu bgyi ba dang/sgom pa yang dag bka' sa lang//bcom ldan 'das kyis rnams grangs du mar/khams rnams kyi rang gi mtshan nyis kyang bka' sa lang/
14a4(1): myi thun pa'i phyogs dang/gnyen po dang/sgom pa dang/ma skyes pa dang/skye pa dangskyes pa rnam par gnas pa dang/myi skyang dang/slar zhing byung pa dang/phel zhing yangs pa nyid kyang bka' sa lang //dran pa nye bar gzhag pa 
14a5(1): de bzhin du/yang dag par spong ba dang/rdzu 'phrul gi rkang pa rnams dang/dbang po rnams dang/stobs rnams dang/byang chub kyi yan lag rnams dang/yan lag 'aka' sa lang/bcom ldan 'odas kyis
14a6(1): rnam grangs du mar bka' sa lang/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa'i rang gi mtshan nyid kyang bka' sa lang/myi thun pa'i phyogs kyi gnyen po dang sgom pa dang/ma skyes pa rnams skye pa dang/skye ba rnams gnas pa 
14a7(1): dang/myi skyab pa dang/slar zhing 'byung ba dang/'phel zhing yangs pa nyid bka' sa lang //bcom ldan 'das kyis chos thams cad kyis/chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid ma mchis pa/chos thams cad ma skyes pa/ma 'gag pa 
14a8(1): bzod ma nas zhi ba/rang bzhin gis yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa/zhes bka' sa lang lag/bcom ldan 'das kyis ci las dgongs nas/chos thams cad ngo bo nyid ma mchis pa/chos thams cad ma skyes ba/ma 'gags pa/
14a9(1): bzod ma nas zhi ba/dang bzhin gis rnam par dag pa/yongs su mya ngan las 'daspa zhes bka' sa lang/snyam bgyid lags te/bcom ldan 'das ci las dgongs nas//chos thams cad ngo bo nyidam mchis/chos thams cad
14b1(1): byang chub sems dpa' don yang dag par 'phags la/'di skad ces bka' stsald to//don dam yang dag 'phags/khyod kyis yongs su rtogs pa dang/tshul bzhin skyes pa legs so legs so//don dam yang dag 'phangs
14b2(1): khyod kyis de ltar skye bo mang po la phan pa dang/skye bo mang po la bde' ba dang/'jig rtend la snying rtse pa dang/lha dang myir bcas pa'i skye dgu'i dang/phan pa dang/bde ba'i phyir bzhugs te/de bzhin gshegs pa la don 'di nyid dri bar 
14b3(1): sems dpa' ni yang khyod legs so//don dam yangadag 'phags/de'i phyir nyon cig/ngas ci las dgongs nas/chos thams cad ngo bo nyid myed pa/chos thams cad ma skyes pa/ma 'gags pa/bzod ma nas zhi ba/rang bzhin gyi
14b4(1): yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa/zhes gsungs pa khyod la bshad par bya'o//don dam yang dag 'phabs/ngas chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid myed pa la rnam pa gsum po 'di lta ste/mtshan nyid kyi ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid dang/brdar rnam par
14b5(1): dag pa'i mtshan nyid yin gyi/don dam pa'i ngo bo nyid myed pa la dgongs nas chos thams cad ngo bo nyid med pa'o zhes stand to//don dam yang dag 'phags/de la chos rnams kyi ngo bo nyid med pa gang zhe na
14b6(1): kun rtand pa'i mtshan nyid gang yin pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar de'i mying dang/rdar rnam par bzhag pa'i mtshan nyid yin gi/mtshan nyid kyi rnam par gnas pa ni ma yin pas/de'i phyir de ni mtshan
14b7(1): nyid ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid ces bya'o//don dam yang dgi 'phags/chos rnams kyi skye ba ngo bo nyid myed pa gang zhe na/chos rnams kyi gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nyid gang yin pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di lta de'i rkyend 
14b8(1): gzhan gis stobs kyis 'byung ba yin gi/bdag nyid kyis ni ma yid bras/de'i phyir de ni skye bo ngo bo nyid myed pa zhes bya'o//don dam yang dag 'apags/chos rnams kyi don dam ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid gang zhe na/rtend cing 'breld par
14b9(1): 'byung ba'i chos gang dag skye bo ngo bo nyid med pa de dag ni/don dam pa'i ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid kyis/ngo bo nyid myed pa yang yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/don dam yang dag 'phags/chos la rnam par dag pa'i dmyigs pa gang
14b10(1): yin pa de ni/ngas don dam pa yin par yongs su stand la/gzhan gi dbang gi mtshan nyad gang yin pa de ni/rnam par dag pa'i dmyigs ma yin pas/de'i phyir don dam pa ngo bo nyid ces bya'o//don dam yang ngag 'phags
15a1(1): //gzhan yang chos rnams kyi yongs su grub pa'i mtshan nyid gang yin pa de yang don dam pa ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid ces bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/don dam yang dag 'phags/chos rnams kyi ngo bo nyid bdag myed pa gang yin pa
15a2(1): ka de ni/de dag gi ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid ce bya'o//de ni don dam pa ni chos rnams kyi ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid kyis kyang rab tu phye ba yin pas/de'i don dam pa ngo bo nyid pa ngo bo nyid ces bya'o//don dam yang dag 'phag sa/de la 'di lta ste dper
15a3(1): na//nam mkha'i me tog ci lta bur ni mtshan nyid myed pa nyid la bya'o//don dam yang dga'aphags/de la 'di lta ste dper na/sgyu byas pa ci lta ba de lta bur ni/skye bo ngo bo nyid myed kyang lta bar bya ba la/don dam yang dag 'phag//
15a4(1): ngo bo nyid myed pa de las cig kyang lta bar bya'o//don dam yang dag dag 'phags/de lta 'di lta ste dper na/nam mkha' gzugs kyi ngo bo nyid myed ba nyid tsam gyis rab tu phye ba dang/thams cad du song pa ci lta ba de ltar bur ni/don dam pa'i ngo bo
15a5(1): nyid myed pa de las chos bdag myed pas rab tu phye ba de las/thams cad du song pa zhig lta bar bya ste/don dam yang dag 'phags/ngo bo nyid rnam pa gsum po de dag la dgongs nas/chos thams cad ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid
15a6(1): bstand to//don dam yang dag 'phags /de la mtshan nyid ngo bo nyid myed pa nyid dgongs nas/ngas chos thams cad ma skyes pa/ma 'gag pa/bzod ma nas zhi ba/rang bzhin gis yongs su mya ngan las 'dar
15a7(1): ba'o/zhes bstan to//de ci'i phyir zhe na/don dam yang dag 'phag sa/'di ltar rang gi mtshan nyid kyis myed pa gang/ma skyes ba gang yin pa de ni/ma 'gags pa yin/ma skyes ma 'gags pa gang yin pa de ni/bzod ma nas
15a8(1): zhi ba yin/bzod ma nas zhi ba gang yin pa de ni/rang bzhin gis yongs su ma ngan las 'das pa de ni/yongs su mya ngan las 'das par bya ba las cung zad kyang myed de/de'i phyir mtshan nyid ngo bo nyid la dgongs nas/ngas chos thos
15a9(1): cad ma skyes pa/ma 'gags pa/bzod ma nas zhi ba'i rang bzhin gis/yongs su mya ngan las 'angs so zhes stand to//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar don dam pa'i ngo bo nyid chos bdag myed pa/rab tu phye ba ni rtag rtag

15b6(1): par zhend to//ci lta ci ltar mngon par zhen pa /de lta de ltar gzhan gi dbang gis/ngo bo nyid la kun tu rtags pa'i/ngo bo nyid du mngon par zhend pa'i rgyu dang/rkyend des phyi ma la gzhan

15b8(1): kun nas nyon mongs par gyur zhing/yun ring por yang sems can dmyal pa rnams sam/yang na dud 'gro rnams sam/yang na yi dgas rnams sam/yang na lha rnams sam/yang na myi rnams kyi nang du

17a1(1): /'di ltar de ni snying rje shind tu chung ba dang/sdug sngal shind tu 'jigs pa'i phyir/rang bzhin Igis rigs dman pa'i dang/bo na yin pa'i phyir ro//de bzhin du ci ltar snying rje chung ba/de ltar seMs

22b10(1): tu dbyangs ba de thob nas de nyid la gnas te/sems kyi rnam ba spangs nas /ci ltar bsams pa'i chos de dag nyid/nang du ting nge 'dzind gi spyod yul/gzugs rnyan du so sor rtog par byed/mos par byed de /de ltar

24b1(1): chos la myi gnas pa la rten nas dad pas rjes su 'brang pa/dbang po rnon por dogs so//bcom ldan 'das la zhi gnas dang/lhag mthong gi ma 'dres pa'i chos la dmyigs pa zhes kyang bgyi'/ma 'dres pa'i myi
24b2(1): la dmyigs pa ni gang lags/byams pa gal te byang chub sems dpa' ci ltar bzung pa dang/gcig tu 'du ba dang/gcig tu rtul ba dang/phung po gcig tu byas te/chos 'di dag thams cad ni de bzhin nyid la gzhol ba
24b3(1): de bzhin nyid la 'bab pa/byang chub la gzhol ba/byang chub la 'bab pa/mya ngan las 'das pa la gzhol ba/mya ngan las 'das pa 'bab pa/ma dan las 'das pa la bab pa/gnas par 'gyur ba la gzhol

24b6(1): dmyigs pa ni gang lags/'dres pa chen por gyurd pa'i chos la dmyigs pa'i chos la dmyigs ba gang lags/'dres pa tshad ma mchis ba ra rig par bya'o//mdo' sde las stsogs pa de dag nyid ci snyed

24b9(1): 'das byang chub sems dpa' ci tsam gis na/'dres ba'i chos la dmyigs ba'i zhi gnas dang/lhag mthong thob bar gyurd nas/byams pa de ni rgyu lngas thob par 'gyur bar rig par bya ste/'di lta ste yid la byed pa'i tshe/
24b10(1): skad cig cig la cig ngan len gi rtend thams cad 'jig par byed pa'i/'dus byas sna tshogs rnam bar spangs te/chos kyi kun dga' la dga' ba thob pa dang/chos snang ba la phyogs bcu' tshad myed cing/rnam pa yongs su ma
25a1(1): //chad pa yang dag par shes pa dang/dgongs pa yongs su grub pa dang/ldan pa rnam par dag pa'i chu dang/mthun pa'i mtshan ma rnam par rtag pa rnams /de la kun tu brtags pa dang/chos sku thob pa dang /
25a2(1): yongs su rdzogs pa dang/yongs su grub par bya ba'i phyir /rgyu gong du ches gong ma /bzang po pas ches bzang po/yang dag par yongs su 'dzind par byed pa'o//bcom ldan 'das 'dres pa'i chos la dmyigs ba'i
25a3(1): zhi gnas dang/lhag mthong de la gang rtogs shing/gang la thob par 'tshal bar bgyi' lags/bka' sacald pa/byams pas dang po rab tu dga' ba la rtogs pa yin la/sa gsum pa 'od byed pa la ni thob par rig par
25a4(1): bya ste/byams pa de ltar mod kyi /byang chub sems dpa' las dang po bas kyang/rjes su slab cing yid la bya pa myi gtong bar bya'o//bcom ldan 'das ci ltar na zhi gnas dang /lhag mthong rtogs pa dang bcan
25a5(1): shing byond pa dang bcas pa'i ting nge 'dzind tu gyurd pa lags/ci ltar na rtog pa dang bcas shing byond pa tsam du 'gyur ba lags/de ltar na rtogs pa ma mchis pa dang/spyod pa ma mchis par 'gyur pa lags
25a6(1): ci ltar gsung zhing rtags pa dang/spyod pa'i chos rnams la /de dag gi mtshan na ma lags shing rags pa dang/myong pa'i chos la spyod pa'i zhi gnas dang/lhag mthong gang yin pa de ni rtogs pa dang bcas shing
25a7(1): spyod pa dang bcas pa'i tinge nge 'dzind yin no//de dag nyid kyi mtshan nyid ma gsal zhing rag las pa myong pa rjes su spyod yul yin mod kyi /'on kyang de snang pa'i 'ol gyi dran pa tsam/phra mo myong pa'i rjes su spyod pa'i zhi
25a8(1): gnas dang/lhag mthong gangayin pa de ni rtog pa myed cing spyod pa tsam gis ting nge 'dzind yin no//de ngag gi mtshan ma la gsal ba'i rag myong pa'i rjes su /spyod yul yin mod kyi thams cad kyi thams cad du/lhun kyis
25a9(1): grub par chos myong pa 'di ni/yid la bya pa rnam par spyod pa'i zhi gnas dang /lhag mthong gang yin pa ni rtog pa myed pa dang/spyod pa myed pa'i ting nge 'dzind yin no//byams pa yongs su tshol ba las /tshol ba las byung
25a10(1): pa bzhi rnams dang/lhag mthong gang yi pa de rnams ni rtog pa dang bcas shing/spyod pa dang bcas pa'i ting nge 'dzind yin na//so sor rtags pa las byung pa gang yin pa de ni/rtog pa myed cing spyod pa can gi ting nge 'dzind
25b1(1): yin no//'dres pa'i chos la dmyigs pa gang yin pa de ni/rtog pa myed pa dang/spyod pa myed pa'i ting nge 'dzind yin no//bcom ldan 'das zhi gnas kyi rgyu mtshan gang lags/rab tu 'dzind pa'i rgyu mtshan ni gang
25b2(1): lags/btang snyoms kyi rgyu mtshan ni gang lags/byams pa sems sgom pa 'am/rgod du gdags pa 'am/kun tu skyo bar 'gyur pa dang/mthun pa'i chos rnams dang/bar chad byed pa'i sems de yin la/byed ba gang yin pa
25b3(1): de ni zhi gnas kyi rgyu mtshan zhes bya'o//byams pa sems bying dam bying du dogs pa na/mngon par dga' bar 'gyur pa dang/mthung ba'i chos rnams dang/sems kyi mtshan ma yid la byed pa de ni rab tu 'dzind pa'i rgyu mtsha na zhes
25b4(1): bya'o//byams pa zhi gnas dang/lhag mthong la cig tu nges pa 'am/zung du 'breld pa'i lam yang rung ste/de gnyi ga'i nye ba'i nyon mongs pas/nye ba'i nyon mongs pas myong pa la sems dang gi dad gis

25b6(1): par rig pa dang/don so sor yang dag par rig pa lags na/ci ltar chos so sor yang dag par rigs pa lags/ci ltar don so sor yang dag par rag pa lags/ci ltar chos so sor yangadag par rig pa la gang zhe na/

26a1(1): //byang chub sems dpa' rnam pa bcus/don so so yang dag par rig pa yin te/bcu gang zhe na/ci snyed yod pa nyid dang/ci lta ba bzhin du yin pa nyid dang/'dzind pa'i don dang/bzung ba'i don dang/gnas kyi

27b1(1): rdzas nyan thos dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyan thos rnams 'jig rten pa dang/'jig rten pas 'das pa mthun mong pa dang mthun mong rung yin pa gang zhig brtan pa/de dag mngon du bya ba gang yin pa'o

28a6(1): byams pa bsaMs pa las byung pa'i shes rab kyis ni/tshig 'bru la gnas/ba kho na yin la/sgra ci bzhin pa ma yin pa dgongs pa can/shes bya'i dngos po dang/cha mthun pa'i ting nge 'dzind gi spyod yul gyi

28b6(1): so so yang dag par rig pas ni /chos dang don kyi mtshan ma thams cad zil kyis non kyis/de ni gang gis kyang zild kyis (ba)nan pa bya bar yin par nga myi smra'o//bcom ldan 'das /bcom ldan 'das kyis

29a3(1): ba dang/gnan du 'gyur ba nyid kyi rgyur yang 'gyur kyi/rjes su 'jug pa'i mtshan nyid gang yin pa de ni mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang/thog ma dang/tha ma myed pa stong pa nyid kyis rnam bar seld to//'dzind pa'i don

29a8(1): na ma gang yin pa de ni/chos 'dus byas stong pa nyid kyis rnam pas seld to//mtshan nyid kyi de bzhin nyid kyi don so so yang dag bar rig par byed pa'i gang zag bdag myed pa mtshan ma dang/chos bdag myed pa'i mtsha

29a10(1): don dam pa stong pa nyid kyi bar gis rnam bar seld to//rnam par dag pa'i de bzhin nyid kyi don dang/so so yang dag par rig par byed pa'i 'dus byas kyi mtshan ma dang/'gyur ba myed cing mtshan ma gang yin ba de ni/
29b1(1): 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid dang/'gyur pa myed pa stong pa nyid kyis rnam par (mnyam pa)gsal to//mtshan ma de'i gnyen por stong pa nyid yid la byed pa'i /stong pa nyid kyi mtshan ma gang yin pa de ni /mtshan nyid myed pa stong pa nyid kyis rnam

30a1(1): /rnam par bral pa nyid dang/de la dmyigs pa gang yin pa de ni/theg pa chen po la stong pa nyid sdus pa zhes bya'o//bcom ldan 'das zhi gnas dang/lhag mthong dag gis ting nge 'dzin

42b10(1): kun rdzob tu na rnam par rtog pa thams cad seld pa'i phyir/dpa'o myi shes pa'i mun pa mun nag seld pa'i phyir/nyi ma blta ba'i bde' ba stond pa'i phyir/zla ba dang 'dra'o//kun rdzob tu na mngon par 'du pa'i phyir/dpa'o dang mtshungs so
43a1(1): //kun rdzob du na sems la dpar byed pa'i phyir tshangs pa dang 'dra'o//kun rdzob du na nam mkha' la dbang byed pa'i phyir//myi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'o//kun rdzob du na thams cad rdzogs song yang/don dam par
43a2(1): na gang du song pa 'am/'ong pa 'am 'dug pa myed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/don dam par na chos thams cad shind tu ma 'byung pa'i phyir ro//lha'i bu bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i
43a3(1): sangs rgyas rin po che tog gi de nyan thos kyi dge' 'dun ni 'dra ste//de bas kyang lhag par bsam gis myi khyab pa'i chos dang yang ldan no//'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pas/myi dmyigs pa'i tshul gis bstand pa 'di bshad pa
43a4(1): na/dge' slong lnga brgya' dang/dge' bsnyend lnga brgya dang/lha'i bu lnga stong dmyigs pa can de dag thams cad kyi sa 'di skad ces gsold to//bcom ldan 'das bdag cag de bzhin gshegs pa dgra gcom pa yang dag par
43a5(1): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas/rin po che tog de'i nyan thos su gyurd cig//'jam dpal gis smras pa/kye grogs po dag sangs rgyas kyi zhing der dmyigs pa can thams cad myi skye'o//kye grogs po dag
43a6(1): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po/dmyigs pa myed pa rnams ni myi skye ba'i tshul gis der skye'o//kye grogs po dang gal te khyed kyang der 'dod na khyed kyis/myi dmyigs pa'i tshul gyis/shes rab
43a7(1): kyi pha rold tu phyind pa la thon bar gis shig//kye grogs po dag khyed nam shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa pa la/myi dmyigs pa'i tshul gis rtson bar gyurd pa'i tshe//grogs po khyed la rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa/
43a8(1): thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes kyang brnyed par myi dka' na//sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par skye pa lta smos kyang mi dgos//lha'i bu rab tu zhi ba rnam par dul ba'i dbang phyug gis yang/'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pa
43a9(1): la 'di skad ces smras so//'jam dpal nyan thos kyi 'dul ba ni ci lta bu//byang chub sems dpa' semasad pa' chen po'i 'dul pa ni ci lta bu/'jam dpal gis smras ba//lha'i bu khams gsum gis skrag pas
43a10(1): 'dul ba ni nyan thos kyi 'dul pa'o//dge' ba'i rtsa ba'i tshogs thams cad bstsags shing/sems can kyi dbugs 'byind ba dang/khams sum du bsam ba bzhin du skye bas 'dul ba ni/kun rdzob du na byang chub sems
43b1(1): dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i 'dul ba'o//nyon mongs pa thams cad dang zhes grags pa ni nyan thos kyi 'dul pa'o//sems can gi nyon mongs pa spangs pa'i phyir /rjes su 'dul pa ni kun rdzob tu na byang chub sems dpa' sems
43b2(1): dpa' chen po'i 'dul pa'o//nyi tshe rig pa'i nyan thos kyi 'dul ba'o//phyogs bcu mtha' yas su myed pa'i 'jig rten gi khams thams cad rig pa ni/kun rdzob du na byang chub sems dpa' chen po'i 'dus pa'o//
43b3(1): bdud thams cad las btang snyoms pa ni nyan thos phyi 'dul ba'o//phyogs bcu tha' yas mu myed pa'i 'jig rtend gi khams su btogs pa'i/bdud kyi khyim thams cad bkrugs shing /phyir rgol ba thams cad gcod pa ni/
43b4(1): kun rdzob tu na byang chub seMms dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'i 'dul pa'o//bdag gi rgyud bsal bar byed pa ni nyan thos kyi 'dul ba'o//sems can thams cad kyi rgyud gsal bar byed cing /thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes
43b5(1): snang bar byed pa dang/chos thams cad la sgrib pa myed pa'i ye shes mthong ba snang par byed pa ni/kun rdzob tu na byang chub sims dpa' seMs dpa' chen po'i 'dul ba'o//rang gi sems kyis bsams
43b6(1): pa ni nyan thos kyi 'dul pa'o//'das pa dang/ma byond pa dang/da ltar gi sangs rgyas thams cad kyis dgongs pa ni//kun rdzob tu na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che na po'i tshul

43b9(1): dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i 'dul pa'o//stobs bcu dang/myi 'jigs pa bzhi dang/ma 'dres pa dang/thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes dang /bral ba ni nyan thos kyi 'dul ba'i//stobs bcu dang /myi 'jigs
43b10(1): ba dang/ma 'dres pa dang /thams cad mkhyend ba'i ye shes su sdus pa ni/kun rdzob du na byang chub sems dpa' sams dpa' chen po'i 'dus pa'o zhes/tshig pa las 'bros pa lta bu'o//pa ni nyan thos kyi
44a1(1): //'dul ba'o//skyed mo 'tshal dang /gzhal myed mkhar dang /lta bus chos kyi dga' ba dga' bas ni/kun rdzob tu na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i 'dul ba'o//bag chags
44a2(1): kyi rgyun ma chad pa ni nyan thos kyi 'dul ba'o//bag chags thams cad yang dag par bcom pa ni/kun rdzob tu na byang 'chub sems dpa' sems dang /dpa' chen po'i 'dul ba'olha'i bu mdor na tshad du btogs pa
44a3(1): tshad kyi chos kyis dran pa ni nyan thos kyi 'dulapa'o//tshad tu btogs pa ma yin pa ni tshad du myed pa'i chos kyi drangs pa ni/kun rdzob tun byang chub sems dpa' chen po'i 'dus pa'otshad yod pa'i
44a4(1): tshul khrims dang/thos pa dang/ting nge 'dzind dang/shes dang dang/rnam par grol pa dang/rnam par grol pa'i ye shes mthong pa thob par byed pa ni nyan thos kyi 'dus pa'otshad yod pa ma yin pa'i tshad
44a5(1): myed pa'i yon tan dang ldan zhing /tshad myed pa'i tshul khrims dang thos ba dang/ting nge 'dzind dang /shes rab dang/rnam par grol ba dang/rnam par grol pa la legs so zhes bya ba byin te'ajam dpal khyod

44a7(1): kyis /kun rdzob tu na byang chub sems dpa'-i sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis 'dul ba legs par bshad pa legs so legs so//'jam dpal yang 'di ltar don 'di nyid stan pa'i phyir khyed la dpes stan to
44a8(1): 'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na/myi nyis shig yod la de la myi cig ni pa lang gi rjes kyi sngags pa smra/myi cig che shos ni rgya tshol tshon po'i sngags pa smra na//'jam dpal de ci snyam du sems/myi des ba lar gyi
44a9(1): sngags pa ci snyed cig brjod nus//'jam dpal gis gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das ba lang gi rjes thams cad kyang chung na/de'i sngags pa lta ci smos//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa
44a10(1): 'jam dpal de bzhin du ba lang gi rjes chung pa ltar /nyan thos kyi 'dul bar lta'o//'jam dpal 'di ci snyam du sems/myi cig shos des rgya mtsho' chen po'i sngags pa ci tsam cig brjod nus//'jam dbal
44b1(1): gyis gsold ba //bcom ldan 'das rgya mtsho'i sngags pa ni shind tu mang ngo//dpag tu ma mchis bsam gyis myi khyab bo//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa //'jam dpal gis de bzhin du rgya

44b3(1): don dbar na /myi skye pa'i tshul gis /bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skyed na//tshig 'di skad ces smras te//bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gis /byang chub sems dpa' sem
44b4(1): dpa' chen po'i slab ba 'di la myi slob pa'i tshul gis slab par bgyi'o//de nas lha'i bu rab tu zhi ba rnam par dul ba'i dbang phyug gis //'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pa la 'di skad ces smras so//ci 'jam

44b9(1): 'gyur /ci ltar na nyon mongs pa yongs su shesapa yin/'jam dpal gis smras pa/don dam par na chos thams cad ma 'byung /ma skyes dngos po myed pa yin/kun rdzob tu na myed pa la phyind ci log du
44b10(1): gyurd te/myed pa la phyind ci log tu gyurd ba /kun tu rtog pa dang /rnam par rtog pa'o//kun tu rtog ba dang rnam par rtog pa de las /tshul bzhin du ma yin pa yid la byed do//tshul bzhin du ma

45b9(1): 'gyur ba ni/don dam par na gang yang myi dmyigs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/lha'i bu don dam par ni shind tu ma 'byung ba'i phyir/chos thams cad ni zhi ba'o//lha'i bu don dam par 'dzind pa byed pa'i

46a4(1): rdzob tu na tshul bzhin ma yin pa yid la byed pa ni 'khor ba'i sgo'o//kun rdzob du na tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa ni mya ngan las 'das pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob du na the tsom ni sgrib pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob tu na rnam
46a5(1): par grol pa ni sgrib pa myed pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob tu ni kun tu rtog pa ni nyon mong sa ba rnams kyi sgo'o//kun rdzob tu na kun tu myi rtog pa ni nyon mongs pa myed pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzab du na rnam par yengs
46a6(1): pa ni rnam par rtog pa rnams kyi sgo'o//kun rdzobs du na zhi gnas ni nye par zhi ba'i sgo'o//kun rdzob tu nalta bar gyurd pa rnams ni nga rgyal gyi sgo'o//kun rdzob du na stong pa nyid ni nga rgyal myed pa'i sgo'o
46a7(1): kun rdzob du na sdig pa'i grogs po dang/stend pa ni/myi dge' ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi sgo'o//kun rdzob tu dge' pa'i shes gnyen dang/stend pa ni dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyi so'o//kun rdzob du na log par

46a9(1): du na 'chal pa ni ngan 'gro tshul khrims ngan pa'i ngan 'gro thams cad kyi sgo'o//kund rdzob tu na tshul khrims ni lha dang phyir skye ba thams cad kyi sgo'o/kun rdzob tu na gnod sems ni byang gzugs ngan pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob
46a10(1): du nas bzod pa ni gzugs phun sum tshogs pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob tu na le lo ni myi rtog pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob du ma tsam 'grus ni rtogs pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzobs tu na rnam par rtog pa ni rgod pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob du
46b1(1): bsam rtand ni sems shind tu sbyangs pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob du na 'chal ba'i shes rab ni khong du myi chung ba'i sgo'o//kun rdzob du ni shes rab ni byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos sum bcu rtsa bdun gi sgo'o//kun rdzob du na
46b2(1): byams pa ni khong khro ba myed pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob tu na snying rje ni lhag pa'i bsam pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob du la lhag pa'i bsam pa ni g.yo' myed pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzong tu na dga' pa ni chos la mos pa'i sgo'o//kun rdzob tu

46b4(1): 'phrul gi rkang ba rnams ni lus dang sems las su chung ba'i sgo'o//kun rjob tu na 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa ni lam ngan pa thams cad las 'das pa'i sgo'o//lha'i bu bzhin yang kun rdzob tu na byang chub kyi seMs ni

47a1(1): //nam mkha' la cha shas dbye' pa gang yang yod dam/lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal myed do//'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu de bzhin du ci ltar nam mkha' la cha shas dbye ba myed pa de bzhin du
47a2(1): ka lha'i bu shind tu ma skyes pa la yang cha shas dbye ba myed do//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam  dpal khyod kyis don dam par na shind tu spyi 'byung ba'i yongs su shes bsam/'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu shind tu myi 'byung ba shin

51b1(1): dmyigs pa'i dug gi skyon chags pa'i slad du/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i le lo'i bar du'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' sa pa yang ba/lha'i bu 'di lta ste dper na/gang myi dbul bo'i kha zam de ni 'khor
51b2(1): lo sgyur ba'i rgyal bo'i dug go//lha'i bu de bzhin du nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas dmyigs pa rnams kyi tshul khrims dang/rtsom 'grus kyi bar du de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' sesams dpa' chen po'i/'chal ba'i
51b3(1): tshul khrims nas le lo'i bar du 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/dmyigs pa'i dug gi skyon lags pa'i phyir ro//lha'i bu 'di lta ste dper /gang bdag nyid sgrub pa'i las byed pa'i skyes bu ni/nor nges grong khyer gi
51b4(1): skye bo thams cad sbyor myi nus so//lha'i bu de bzhin du gang nyan thos dang/rang sangasargyas dmyigs pa can dbul ba dang/dman pa'i sems dang dmyigs pa'i sems dang/ldan pa rnams kyi thon 'grus kyi bar gis/'dzam bu
51b5(1): 'i gling gi sems can yongs su thard byed kyang myi nus na/sems can thams cad lta ci smos/lha'i bu 'di lta ste dper na/nor che ba long spyod che ba/gtong pa dang ldan pa byams pa la gnas ba/snying rje chen po dang ldan pa/
51b6(1): ded dpon chen pos sems can thams cad/bla na myed pa'i bde' bar byed pa la zhugs pa ni/nor de grangs myed dpag tu myed pa'i sems can bde' bar byed do//lha'i bu de bzhin du dgang byang chub
51b7(1): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po lhag pa'i bsam ba la zhugs pa/sems can thams cad la shes rab dang/thabs dang/snying rtse ba chen pos skuld pa/skald pa chen po bye ba khrag khrig brgya' stod grangs myed bar
51b8(1): bsod nams dang/ye shes kyi tshogs yang dag par grub pa ni/nor chen po myi dmyigs par thon 'grus kyi bar de dag gis/sems can grangs myed dpag tu myed ba yongs su smyind par byed do//myi bzhog pa'i tshub
51b9(1): gyis rnam pa thams cad mkhyend pa nyid la 'god par byed do//de nas bcom ldan 'das la/tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen pos 'di skad ces gsold to//bcom ldan 'das nyan thos kyi 'dus ma byas kyis chos rnams
51b10(1): mngon gsum du bgyid la/bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che po ni 'dus byas su btogs pa lags na/ci ltar 'dus ma byas su btogs pa'i nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas dmyigs pa can thams cad
52a1(1): //zild kyis gnon/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa/'od srung de'i don rab tu grub par bya ba'i phyir dper byas ste/skyes bu mkhas pa smra pa'i don rig pa la dpes de'i don go bar 'gyur te//'di lta ste dper na/
52a2(1): rgya mtsho chen po'i gzhi' mar gyis gang bar 'gyur la/de nas myi la las skra brgyad bshags pa'i rtse mo'i thisga bas sam/de dag las mar gi thigs pa cig blangs na/'od srung de ci snyam tu sems /skra brgyar bshags pa'i rtse mo'i thigs pa
52a3(1): mar gi thigs pa blangs pas/rgya mtsho chen po'i gzhi mar gis gang ba zild kyis gnon par nus sam/'od srung gis gsold ba/bcom ldan 'das de lta ma lags so//bde' par gshegs pa de lta ma lags so//bcom ldan
52a4(1): 'das kyis bka' stsald pa yang 'od srung de dag las ches mang pa dang/ches mchog tu gyurd pa dang/ches dam pa dam pa dang/ches rin po che gang yin/'od srung gis gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das 'di ltar rgya mtsho' chen po cig gi
52a5(1): bye ba stong 'phrag rgya'i cha shas de yang /bcom ldan 'das shind tu mar/shind tu mchog/shind tu dam pa/shind tu rin po che lags ste//bcom ldan 'das rgyar bshags pa'i skra'i rtse mo'i thigs sam/mar gyi
52a6(1): thigs pa blangs pa de lta ma lags na/bcom ldan 'das rgya mtsho' chen po zhig lta ci smos//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald ba/'di lta ste dper na /rgyar bshags pa'i skra'i rtse ma'i thol mos mar gi thigs

52a8(1): du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dmyigs pa myed pa/dad pas spyod ba'i sa la spyi spyod pa'i tsha rnam pa'i mchog thams cad dang/ldan ba'i bsod nams dang/ye shes kyi tshogs thams cad yang dag par sgrub ba/'dus ma byas
52a9(1): su phyi lta bu'i tshul gis lta'o//de yang don dam par na me 'phar bas yongs su smind par byed bas /rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang/ldan pa'i thams cad mkhyend ba'i ye shes su myi dmyigs pa'i tshul gis 'gyur ro//
52a10(1): 'od srung de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' chen po yang /'dus ma byas thob ba'i nyan thos dang/ngang sangs rgyas dmyigs pa tsan thams cad /myi dmyigs pa'i tshul gis bzild kyis gnon no//'od srung 'di lta ste/dper na
52b1(1): grong khyer chen por chung ba'i mching nor bu khur rgya stong du gyurd pa dang/rgya mtsho' chen po na yod pa'i gru chung rin po che bye ru r+ya nor bu rin po che chen po de pade zhing nye zho myed par 'dzam bu'i gling du phyind na 'di ci snyam du sems /grong khyer
52b2(1): chen pos chung pa'i mching bus nor bus gru'i nang du bcug pas /rin che pa'i bye du rtsa nor bu rin po che zil kyis gnon bar nus sam/'od srung gis gsold pa/bcom ldan 'das de ltar ma lags so//bde' bar shegs pa
52b3(1): de ltar ma lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa /'od srung ci ltar grong khyer chen por chud pa'i mching bu'i nor bu rin po che lta bur/'dus ma byas mthong ba'i nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas dmyigs pa'i can
52b4(1): thams cad lta bar bya'o//'od srung ci ltar nor bu rin po che chen por bye ru rgya rgya mtsho' chen po'i nang na yong ba grur btsug pa /bde' na nye zho myed bar 'tshong khang gi bar du 'ongs par gyurd ba /de lta bur ni 'od
52b5(1): srung byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'khor pa'i grong khyer gi tshong khang gi bar du 'ongs bas /sems can thams cad /bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu myi 'jog pa'i tshul gis bar
52b6(1): du 'god par bya'o//'od srung 'di lta ste dper na/dpyid zla tha chungs kyi zla ba la sa chen po lo thogs kyis rab tu gang pa la/grog sbur phra mo zas kyi 'bru cig khyer ba dang/sa chen po 'di de dag gis gang ba de nyis gang mang bar
52b7(1): sems/'od srung gis gsold ba/bcom ldan 'das lo thog gis ltam ba'i sa chen po de las dpag du myed/ma mchis pa'i 'bru'i phung po chen po 'byung pas 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald ba/'od srung
52b8(1): ci ltar grog sbur phra mos khyer ba'i zas kyi 'bru de lta bu ni/'od srung nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas dmyigs pa can thams cad kyis /zag pa myed pa'i rnam par grol ba'i 'bras bur lta'o//'od srung ci ltar lo thog mang
52b9(1): pos rab tu gang ba'i sa de lta bu ni/'od srung byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spyi gnas pa'i tshul gis kun rjod pa dang/don dam pa'i bden pa la gnas pas/pha rold tu phyind pa drug las stsogs pas/sangs rgyas
52b10(1): kyi chos thams cad dang /bla na myed par sdus ba'i bsod nams dang/ye shes kyi tshogs kyi dge' ba'i rtsa ba lo thog lta bu'o//de yongs su smind par gyurd nas 'di lta ste/bla na myed pa rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan ba
53a1(1): / O /ye shes kyi bden pa myi dmyigs pa'i tshul gis sems can thams cad kyi mtsho' par 'gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen pos 'di skad ces gsold to//bcom ldan 'das
53a2(1): ngo mtshard to//bde' bar gshegs pa ngo mtshard to//bcom ldan 'das kyis myi 'chang pa'i tshul gis ci ltar bshad pa de bzhin no//bcom ldan 'das de ltar rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa /bla na ma mchis
53a3(1): pa myi dmyigs pa'i ye shes kyis /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po mos pas spyod pa'i sa la /myi spyod pa'i tshul gis spyod pas/nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas dmyigs pa can thams cad zild kyis gnon to/
53a4(1): de nas gang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rin po che tog gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing nas /'phags pa 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pa dang/lhan cig tu/'jig rten gi khams myi mjed ces bya ba 'dir 'ongs pa'i /byang chub 
53a5(1): sems dpa' sems dpa' chon po de dag stan ba 'di thos nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di ces gsold to//bcom ldan 'das'di lta ste/nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas dmyigs pa can kun rdzob /nyon

53a7(1): myi gda' ste/bcom ldan 'das de ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi dag pa'i bka' mchid myi thos pa'i tshul gis thos so//phyir myi ldog pa'i bka' mchid ma thos pa'i tshul gis thos so//
53a8(1): myi dmyigs pa'i bka' mchid myi thos ba'i tshul gis thos so/ O /don dam par na chos thos thams cad shind tu ma skyes pa'i bka' mchid myi thosapa'i tshul gis thos so//don dam par chos thos thams cad shin tu
53a9(1): ma 'byung ba'i bka' mchid myi thos pa' tshul gyis thos so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shag kya thub pa ni dga' pa mdzad de/gang de rnyed kyi nyam nga pa bzod
53a10(1): cing gang chos thams cad don dam par na shind tu ma skyes pa la/chos rnams rab 'bring tha mar stond par mdzad do//'di lta ste/don dam par na shind du ma skyes pa dang/ma 'byung ba dang/dngos po ma mchis pa'i
53b1(1): rnam pa ste/bcom ldan 'das de ltar don dam par chos thams cad rnam par gcig pa las/sems can rnams kyi dmyigs pa gzhom pa'i slad du/myi stond pa'i tshul gis rnam pa du mar chos stond to//
53b2(1): de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de rnams kyis/lha'i me tog dang/spos dang/bdug pa dang/'phreng ba dang/byug pa dang/phye ma dang/chos gos dang/gdugs dang/rgyal 'tshan dang
53b3(1): ba dan dang/rnam par rgyal pa'i ba dan dang bcas pa'i bla res/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shag kya thub pa la bla na myed pa'i mchog byas nas/
53b4(1): 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pa la 'di skad ces smras so//'jam dpal bdag 'jig rtend gi khams rin po che dang ldan pa zhes bya ba 'ongs so//'jam dpal gis smras pa/rigs kyi bu
53b5(1): khyed nga de'i dus la bab par shes na dor shig//byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po de dag gis smras pa/'jam dpal ci khyod myi 'gro 'am/'jam dpal gis smras pa/rigs kyi bu
53b6(1): sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing gcig go//de bzhin du sangs rgyas ni sangs rgyas gcig go//de bzhin du chos thams cad chos gcig go//de bzhin tu sems can thams cad ni sems can
53b7(1): gcig go//de dag 'di las khyad par du ci yod na bdag der 'gro/de skad ces smras pa/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag gis smas pa/'jam dpal ci ltar sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams chad sangs
