53b9(1): gis smras pa/rigs kyi bu dag sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams can ni zhing ma yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/don dam par shind tu ma skyes pa'i phyir ro//rigs kyi bu dag sangs rgyas
53b10(1): don dam par na shind tu ma byung pa'i phyir ro//rigs kyi ba dag chos thams cad ni chos ma yin no//de ci'i phyi zhe na/don dam par dngos po myed pa'i phyir ro//rigs kyi pu dag sems can thams cad ni sems can ma yin no
54a1(1): //de ci'i phyir zhe na/don dam par na shind tu ma byung pa'i phyir ro//de nas de'i tshe 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pas ci nas stong sum gi stong chen po 'jig rtend gi khams 'di/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rin po che'i 
54a2(1): tog gis 'jig rten gi khams rin po che dang ldan pa zhes bya ba /de ci srid 'dra bar lhan cig myed par snang pa de lta bur bsam pa bsams nas/de lta dra bar ting nge 'dzind la snyoms par zhugs so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems
54a3(1): dpa' chen po de dag gis /bcom ldan 'das de bzhinagashegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rin po che tog de dang/'jig rtend gi khams rin po che dang lngan pa zhes bya ba de 'ang mthong ste/byang chub sems dpa' seMs
54a4(1): dpa' chen po de dag 'di snyam du/'jig rtend gi khams ring po che dang ldan pa zhes ba par phyind bar sems so//de dag gis bcom ldan 'das de bzhinadagashegs pa dgra bcom pa la yang dag par rdzogs pa'is sangs rgyas shag kya thub

54a6(1): du shes so//de nas byang chub seMs dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag/bcom ldan 'das de bzhig gshegs pa shag kya thub pa la /rin po che tog snyam byed nas 'di skad ces gsold to//bcom ldan 'das
54a7(1): bdag cag 'jig rtend gi khams rinapo che dang ldan pa zhes bya ba 'dir sus phyind par bgyis/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa shag kya thub bas bka' stsald pa/rigs kyi bu dag khyed sus khrid byang chub sems dpa' chen po
54a8(1): de dag gis gsold pa/bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pas khrid do//bcom ldan 'das shag kya thub pas bkal sam lad pa/rigs kyi bu khyed ni/'jam dpal gzho nur gyud pas /ma 'ongs pa ma song pa'i tshul gis phyind
54a9(1): par byas so//de nas 'jam dpal gzho nur rgyurd pas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chenapo de dag la 'di skad ces smras so//rigs kyi bu dag khyed gang du khrid/gang du phyind par byas pa mnyam par bzhag par ltos shig//
54a10(1): de nas byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po de dag gis/rang rang gi ting nge 'dzind la mnyam par bzhag ste/ltas na 'jig rtend gi khams (mi jed)de nyid nas bdag nyid gang du yang ma g.yos par mthong ste/ngo mtshard du gyurd /rmad du gyurd nas 'di ltar
54b1(1): bdag cag stand 'di las/ma 'phags par 'jig rtend gi khams rin po che dang ldan pa zhes bya bar yang phyind pa snyam byed do//'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd ba'i byind rlabs bsam gis spyi khyab po zhes/ched du brjod pa ched du brjod do//de dag gis
54b2(1): yang smras pa/bcom ldan 'das sems can thams cad kyis spyi dmyigs pa'i tshul gis byin kyi rlabs 'di thos par gyurd cig//de nas bcom ldan 'das shag kya thub pas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag la bka'
54b3(1): sa lang ba/rigs kyi bu dag 'di lta ste dper na/nam mkha' ni sa'i snod dang/shing gi snod dang/khar ba'i snod dang/zas kyi snod dang/dung gi snod dang/gser gi snod dang/nor bu dang/mu tig dang/bye du bya dang/dung dang/ma shel

54b5(1): nam mkha' la tha dad pa myed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/don dam par nam mkha' shind tu myid cing shind tu dngos po myed pa'i phyir te/de bzhin du du rigs kyi bu dag don dam par chos thams cad shind tu ma skyes ma byung
54b6(1): dngos po myed mod kyis tha snyad/'jig rtend kun rdzob kyi tha snyad du 'dogs shing rab tu 'jog ste/'di ni bzugs so/'di ni tshor pa'o'di ni 'du shes so/'di ni 'du byed rnams so//'di ni rnam par shes pa'o
54b7(1): 'di dag ni skye mched rnams so//'di dag ni khams rnams so//'di ni sangs rgyas dkon mchog go//'di ni chos dkon mchog go//'di na dge' 'dun dkon mchog go//'di nyin thos kyi theg pa'o//
54b8(1): 'di ni rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa'o//'di ni bla na myed pa'i theg pa chen po'o//'di dag ni dge' ba bcu'i las kyi lam rnams so//'di dag ni spyi dge' pa bcu'i las kyi lam rnams sa/'di ni sems can dmyal pa'o
54b9(1): 'di dag ni dur 'gro'i skye gnas so//'di dag ni yi dgas kyi yul lo//'di dag ni lha ma yin no//'di dag ni spyi'o//'di dag ni lha'o//'di dag ni 'dod pa na spyod pa'i lha'o//'di dag ni bzugs na spyod pa'i
54b10(1): lha'o//'di dag ni bzug myed pa na spyod pa'i lha'o/'di dag ni 'dod pa'i khams so//'di ni gzugs kyi khams so//'di ni gzungs myed pa'i khams so//'di na lus kyi legs par spyod pa'o//
55a1(1): //'di ni ngag gi legs par spyod pa'o /'di ni yid kyi legs par spyod pa'o//'di ni sbyind pa'i pha rold tu phyind pa'o/'di ni tshul khrims kyi pha rold tu phyind pa'o//'di ni bzod pa'i pha rold tu phyinadapa'o/
55a2(1): 'di ni brtson 'grus kyi pha rold tu phyind ba'o/'di ni bsam rtand gi pha rold tu phyind pa'o/'di ni shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa'o //'di ni thabs kyi pha rold tu phyind pa'o/'di ni smon lam gi pha rold tu phyind ba'o //
55a3(1): 'di ni stobs kyi pha rold tu phyinadapa'o //'di ni ye shes kyi pha rold tu phyind pa 'o//'di ni sa bcu' dang /dbang bcu' dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i stobs bcu dang /myi 'jigs pa bzhi dang /
55a4(1): so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi' dang /dbang po lnga dang /stobs lnga dang /ci tsam du sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad dang /ci tsam du rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i thams cad mkhyend pa'i ye shes

55a8(1): skyes ma 'byung pa /dngos po myed pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag shind tu ngo mtshard du gyurd nas/stand las langs te/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa
55a9(1): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shag kya thub pa la 'ang rgya che ba/shind tu khyad par dang ldan pa'i mchog byas nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo' bos phyag 'tshal te/tshig 'di skad ces gsol to
55a10(1): bcom ldan 'das sems can thams cad 'di lta bu'i /seng ge'i sgra/bye brag tu 'phags pa'i sgra/khyu mchog gyi sgra/skyes bu chen po'i sgra/de kho na'i sgra/yang dag pa'i sgra/myi dmyigs pa'i sgra/
55b1(1): don dam pa'i sgra/myi dmyigs pa'i tshul gis 'byind par gyurd cig//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag dga' mgur rangs nas/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom 
55b2(1): pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shag kya thub pa la lan stong du mar skor pa byas nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi gnas nas lhar 'ongs te/de dag phyogs bcur song nas/myi dmyigs pa'i rtson 'grus kyis
55b3(1): go cha chen po go cha myed pa'i tshul gis slob nas/don dam par na kun rdzob kyi bden pa gnyis la gnas pa'i chos stand de la 'dug ste/ma langs par 'di 'dra ba'i ting nge 'dzind la mnyam par bzhag ste/ting nge 'dzind

78b1(1): shes shing mdzes la yon tan kun ldan 'gyur//srog chags rgya 'phrag du mas mdun bdar cing//zla ba gsum du rgyal pa de mchod nas //de ni srog chags drug stong sum rgya' dang//lhan cig khyis gyi 
78b2(1): nang nas 'byung 'gyur zhing//rgyal ba myi 'phan bzhugs dang 'das ba'i //chos kyi tshul yang kun tu 'dzind par 'gyur //de 'dra'i dpa' po de nas shi 'phos nas//bde' ba can gi 'jig rten
78b3(1): khams su 'gro//blo ldan des ni 'od dpag myed ba dang//gzhan yang phyogs bcu' mtha' yas bzhugs pa'i//sangs rgyas gang 'ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed//mthong zhing snyend bkur byed bar 'gyur /
78b4(1): ting 'dzind mngon shes brtson 'grub stobs thob cing//bskald pa bzang la gang dag phyir 'byung ba'i//rang 'byung 'jig rten rnams la phan rtson pa//'jig rten mgon de dag la yang snyen bkur byed/
78b5(1): bye mang skald bar de dag mchod byas te//sems can bye bar yongs su smind byas shing//skald pa bzang po bye ba stong phrag brgya' bcu' na//rgyal ba zla ba mchog ces bya bar 'gyur /zla mchog
78b6(1): rkang nyis gtso bo'i mdzod spu nas //'od zer gser mdo/ga shind tu yid 'ong ba'i//gang dag 'byung bar gyurd pa de dag gi//'jig rten khams de yongs su gsal bar gyurd/
78b7(1): gang phyir 'od kyis 'jig rten de khyab bas //nyi zla me dang nor bu'i 'od rnams ni//snang bar myi 'gyur rgyu skar tshogs myin zhig//nyin dang mtshan yang 'grang du yod pa myin//de tshe dang 
78b8(1): sangs rgyas dag yod myin zhing//nyan thos rnams myed mu stegs can//tshogs myed mnyam myed de'i dge' 'dun rnam dag cing //bud myed rnam par spangs pa nyid du 'gyur//myi rnams thams 
78b9(1): cad gser gi mdog 'dra zhing//bsod nams rgya'i mtshan can lta na sdug//mdal na myi gnas 'dod pa myi sten cing//pad ma'i nang nas rdzus te skyes shing 'byung//rgyal sras rnams kyi tshogs
78b10(1): 'byung gang yin pa//de ni bgrang pas rtogs par myi nus shing//thams cad mngon shes thub pa dal spyod dang//myi skye ba zod cing mnyam myed chags pa myed //de na bdud myed sems can yod
79a1(1): ///myin zhing/bslab ba 'dra myed phyogs bcu 'jig rten yod ma yin//'jig rten khams 'di dag cing dga' ldan gi //nye bar spyod pa'i rnam ba 'dra bar 'gyur//gser rdul mu tig dam ba'i
79a2(1): 'dra mchog gis//sbras shing gtsang la dri myed yangs pa yin//rgyal ba de'i sku tshe lo grangs ni//stong phrag phye ba bcu gsum 'gyur//stond pa de'i de bzhin gshegs 'gyur nas//de tshe dam chos skald
79a3(1): ba cig gnas shing//bzhugs bzhin mya ngan 'das na yang de ni//dam chos mdzod la nyams bar 'gyur ba myed//gal te rgyal ba dang ni zhing de'i//phun gsum tshogs pa'i skald bar brjod byas kyang//
79a4(1): rgya mtsho' dag la chu thigs cig bzhin du//dpe' yang brjod par 'gyur pa ma yin no//de nas bu mo zla mchog gis /bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar /bdag nyid mngon gsum du lung stand pa thos nas/dga' mgu'
79a5(1): mchog tu dga' ba de nyid kyis /shing ta la bdun tsam srid du nam mkha' la mngon bar 'phags so//mngon par 'phags ma thag tu /bu mo de'i bur myed kyi lus mngon ba cig/skyes ba'i lus mngon
79a6(1): bar grub par gyurd pa dang/'jig rten du sa chen po yang g.yo sa par gyurd /me tog gi char dag kyang rab tu g.yos//lha dang myi'i bsil snyan 'bum phrag du ma dag kyang sgra khrold par gyurd to//
79a7(1): 'jig rten kun tu snang pas khyab par yang gyurd to//'jig rtend kun tu snang pa chen pos khyab par gyurd to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' zla mchog gis bar snang de nyid la 'jug bzhin du /phyogs bcu'i sems can
79a8(1): lus rnams cig du 'gyur zhing //zhugs ni chab 'gyur gda'//mnyam myed thub dbang yon tan 'byung ba'i gnas//'gro la phan zhes log pa'i tshig myi gsung//bar snang dogs kun tu 'dzind 'gyur zhing
79a9(1): yungs 'bru'i nang du zhing rgya chud 'gyur la//gser ma zhags pas bzung ba rdo thog gda'//rgyal ba log pa'i tshig ni gsung myi 'gyur//de 'drar yang dag de bzhin nyid gsung ba'i//rgyal ba des bdag byang chub
79a10(1): nges stand cing//gang phyir dogs kun tu rab g.yos pas//byang chub dam pa thob 'gyur gor ma bkur//sangs rgyas theg mchog bdag lung stand bas na//rang 'byung gi ni ci skar gsungs de dang/sngan chad skal
79b1(1): rgyang gang 'tshal ba //de ltar bdag gis chos mchog 'khor lo bskor//lha myi lha myin gnod sbyin dge' slong dang//dge' snyen rnams dang byang chub sems dpa' dag/thams cad nyid myed mthu' stobs 
79b2(1): ldan ba'i//bder gshegs 'gyur gyis som nyi ma byed cig/chos 'di thams cad sgyu ma lta bu ste//rgyal ba rnams kyi long spyod myi 'dra zhing//bdag myed gsol ba shed bu dga' yang myed//sems can srog 
79b3(1): myed gang zag myed bar gsung//bzod ldan ngo bo nyid kyi chos rnams kun//rang bzhin dag cing ngo bo nyid myed de//bdag sngon bud myed rang bzhin gyurd pa gang//de ni dngos myed gsog stong ya ma rla//phyi ma 
79b4(1): dngos po de yang stong zhing skad cig ste//dngos myed myi g.yo' gsog dang ya ma rla//rtog dang rnam par rtog dang bral gyurd cing//rtog 'gyur de yang mkhas la rjes bzhin gang zhig myed byang chub 'tshang rgya zhing//
79b5(1): thul dka' btud kyang rab tu gzhom pa dang//stong sum chen po'i 'jig rten khams su ni//chos kyi 'khor lo dam pa skor 'dod pa//de ni rtag par byang chub sems skyed la//de bzhin gshegs 
79b6(1): pa rab tu mchod bgyis na//de ni myur tu yon tan 'byung ba'i gnas//stond ba rgyal pa sdig myed lam rtogs 'gyur//skyes bu'i rtsal 'phags gsung snyan mthar byon na//rgyal ba myi mchog gtso' 
79b7(1): la phyag 'tshal lo//dga' 'dzind dag ba'i dngos po stsold mdzad pa'i//thub pa chos 'byig 'byung gnas dbang phyug stobs/bde' ba'i 'byung gnas bde' kun rab gsol ba//de bzhin gshegs pa 
79b8(1): bdud kyi tsher ma dkrugs//stobs 'os rnams kyi stod pa gang zag mchog/rang 'byung dbang ldan sred bral bstod par bgyi'//bdag gis phyogs su ltam na de dag tu//sdig myed sangs rgyas rgya 'phrag bsam 
79b9(1): yas mthong//shag kya'i seng ge ci lta de lta bur/phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas de dag bdag lung stond//rgyal ba thams cad chos nyid sangs rgyas cig/de bzhin chos kyang de bzhin nyid kyis gcig/sems can 
79b10(1): rnams kyang yang dag thar nges par//gang la bzod 'di mchis par rgyal par 'gyur//de nas byang chub sems dpa' zla mchog gyis/tshigs su gcad pa de dag gis smras pa nas/bar snang de la babs te/bcom ldan 
80a1(1): //'das kyi zhabs gnyis la rab tu gtugs te/de nas rab tu gtugs ma thag tu /sangs rgyas 'brum 'phrag mang po dag kyang snang par gyurd te//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis kyang /de bla na myed
80a2(1): pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu lung stand to//de sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade dag mngon gsum du mthong zhing /bdag nyid lung stan pa yang thos nas/dga' zhing mgu' la yid rangs ste/rab tu dga' zhing dga'
80a3(1): ba dang/yid bde' ba skyes nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar rab tu byung bar gsol ba btab ste//bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba la rab tu byung bar gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis
80a4(1): bka' stsald pa/khye'u de'i phyir pha ma dag gnang bar byed du cug shig/de nas khye'u de'i pha ma dag gis cho 'phrul chen po de dag mthong zhing/de mngon sum du lung stan pa yang thos na/'di skad ces gsol to//
80a5(1): bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gis /khye'u 'di rab tu byung bar gnang lags so//bdag cag gis kyang chos de dag slad kyis thos par bgyi lags so//zhes gsold pa dang/bcom ldan 'das
80a6(1): khye'u de rab tu byung ngo//srog chags khris kyang bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skyed do//chos kyi rnam grangs 'di bshad pa na/lha dang myi khrag khrig 'phrag bdun /chos rnams
80a7(1): la chos kyi myig rdul myed cing dri ma dang bral ba rnam par dag go//dge' slong lnga rgya ni lend pa myed par zag pa las sems rnam par grol lo//sngon byang chub sems dpa' ma skyes pa'i dge' slong ma gnyis brgya' dang
80a8(1): srog chags nyi khris ni bla na myed pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i byang chub du sems skyed do//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsald pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' zla mchog dang/tshe dang ldan pa kun dga'
80a9(1): bo dang/byang chub sems dba' de dag dang/nyan thos de dag dang/thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de dag dang/lha dang/myi dang/lha ma yin dang/dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yid rangs te/bcom ldan 'das

80b1(1): /rgya gar skad du /a r+ya tsa tu k+sha nir ha ra /na ma ma ha ya na su tra//bod skad du //'phags pa gzhi sgrub pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo'//sangs rgyas dang //
80b2(1): byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus cig na/bcom ldan 'das gnyan yod na rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi 'tshal/'gon myed zas sbyin gi kun dga' ra ba na
80b3(1): dge' slong lnga rgya' tsam gi dge' slong gyi dge' 'dun chen po dang/byang chub sems dpa' brgya stong thams cad dang/go cha chen po bgos pa sha stag dang/'dod pa spyod pa dang/gzugs na spyod pa'i lha'i bu rnams dang

80b5(1): 'das la mchod pa'i las kyi phyir /rin po che'i gdugs dpag tshad bcu'i tshad tsam thogs te/bcom ldan 'das kyi dbu'i gtsug tu 'dzind to//de nas 'khor de nyid du dga' ldan gi rigs kyi lha
80b6(1): 'i bu dpal bzangs ldan zhes bya ba/bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las phyir ma ldog pa/g.yog dang bcas te /'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyurd te /'dus pa de stan las lang
80b7(1): te bla gos phrag ba cig tu bzar nas /pus so g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pa ga la ba de log sa su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/'jam dpal gzho nur rgyurd pa la 'di skad
80b8(1): ces smras so//'jam dpal khyod da dung de bzhin gshegs pa la mchod pa'i las mdzad pas mchi' 'am/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lha'i bu 'di ci snyam du sems/rgya mtsho' chen po chu thams cad
80b9(1): lend pas zhar yang mchog shes sam//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal de ni ma lags so/'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu de bzhin du /rgya mtsho' gting dpag dka' ba de lta bu thams cad
80b10(1): mkhyend pa'i ye shes tshad myed/dpag tu myed pa btsal bar byas ste/byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa la/mchod pa'i las kyis zhar yang tshim par myi bya'o//lha'i bus smras pa /
81a1(1): //'jam dpal byang chub sems dpa' ci la dmyigs te/de bzhin gshegs pa la mchod bar bgyi'/'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu byang chub sems dba' dmyigs pa bzhis /de bzhin
81a2(1): gshegs pa la mchod par bya'o//bzhi gang zhe na 'di lta ste/thams cad mkhyend pa nyid la dmyigs pa dang/sems can thams cad rab tu grol bar dmyigs pa dang/dkon mchog gsum gi gdung myi bcor
81a3(1): bar bya ba la dmyigs pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan bkod pa yongs su bzung ba la dmyigs pa ste/lha'i bu byang chub sems pas /dmyigs pa bzhi bo de dag gis/de bzhin gshegs pa la 'chod bar bya'o/
81a4(1): lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal khyod kyis tshangs pa'i rigs kyi lha'i bu nang du 'dus ba/tshangs pa gtsug phud can la/byang chub sems dpa'i lam bzhi sgrub pa'i/chos kyi rnam grangs bshad pa de/'jam
81a5(1): dpal legs bar bshad du gsol//de la bdag dang thams cad dang ldan ba'i 'khor 'di yang gnyan par 'tshal lo/'jam dpal byang chub sems dpa' rnams la/chos kyi ser sna ma
81a6(1): mchis/slob dpon gi chos kyi dpe' 'khrud kyang ma mchiso/'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu de'i phyir legs par rab tu nyon la yid la zung shig dang/bzhi ba sgrub pa zhes bya ba'i
81a7(1): chos kyi rnam grangs de nges par rang bshad do//lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi/lhag pa'i bsam ba'i sems skyed par rig bar bya'o//bzhi gang zhe na/lha'i bu 'di la byang chub
81a8(1): sems dpa' sems can dpag tu myed pa/sdu ba'i phyir sems skyed pa dang/sems can yongs su smyind pa dpag tu myed pa /yongs su smind par bya ba'i phyir sems skyed pa dad/dge' ba'i rtsa ba dpag
81a9(1): tu myed pa bsags ba'i phyir sems skyed pa dag/sangs rgyas kyi chos dpag tu myed pa /mngon bar rdzogs par rtogs par bya ba'i phyir sems bskyed pa ste/lha'i bu bzhi' bo de dag ni byang chub sems pa' lhag pa'i
81a10(1): bsam ba'i phyir sems skyed bar rig par bya'o//lha'i bu bzhi bo de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sems skyed brag cha lta bu yin no//bzhi gang zhe na/slong ba rnams la khong khro ba myed pa'i sems dang
81b1(1): log par zhugs pa rnams la snyid rtse ba'i sems dang/shes rab kyis sa nyams pa'i sems dang/rtson pa nyam 'og tu chud pa'i sems te/lha'i bu bzhi bo de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sems skyed pa

81b4(1): sems skyed pa/stan pa snying po can myi phyed pa/rdo rje lta bu 'o//bzhi gang zhe na/bsam ba las myi phyed pa dang/mdza' ba las myi phyed pa dad/nan tan las myi phyed pa dang/theg pa chen po las myi phyed ba ze
81b5(1): bzhi bo de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi bo de dag ni /byang chub sems dpa' rnas kyi sems skyed pa rnyed par dka' ba yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/bzhi gang zhe na/nyon mongs ba dang/ma 'dres ba dang
81b6(1): brnyed pa dang/bkur sti dang/tshigs su gcad pa thams cad dang/ma 'dres ba dang/theg pa chen bo dang/ma 'dres ba dang /skye bo myi grungs pa thams cad dang/'dres ba ste/bzhi bo de dag
81b7(1): lha'i bu bzhi po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams kyi sems skyed pa bla na myed pa'o//bzhi gang zhe na /'phags pa'i dngos po thams cad yongs su gtong ba'i sems dang/byin kyis
81b8(1): rlob pa'i sems dang/byin nas 'gyod pa myed pa'i sems dang/rnam par smyind pa la re ba myed pa'i sems dang/byang chub tu yongs su sngo' ba'i sems te/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu ba zhi po 'di dag
81b9(1): ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi spyi bor phyind ba'i chos so//bzhi gang zhe na/shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa dang/thabs la mkhas pa dang/dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzind pa dang/sems can yongs
81b10(1): su smyind par byed pa dang/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub semasad pa' rnams kyi/byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi lam stond par byed ba'o//bzhi gang zhe na/pha rold

82a4(1): 'di dag ni/byang chub ses dpa' rnams kyi mos pa'o//bzhi gang zhe na/khyim par gyurd na bdag gi longs spyod kyis mchog shes pa dang/gzhan gi nor la mngon bar 'dod pa myed pa dang/rab tu byung
82a5(1): na 'phags ba'i rigs kyis chog shes pa dang/sbyang ba'i yon tan dang yod byar nyung pa ste/bzhi po de dag gi//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di ni/byang chub sems dpa'i rnams kyi sbyin pa'o//
82a6(1): bzhi gang zhe na/chos kyi sbyin ba dang/zang zing gi sbyin pa dang shog shog dang/snags sa dang/smyu gu dang/legs bam du sbyin pa dang/chos smra ba la bsam pa thag pa nas legs so zhes
82a7(1): bya ba sbyin ba ste/bzhi bo de dag go//lha'i bu ba zhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi snying po nyid do//bzhi gang zhe na/thos pa la nan tan snying po dang/longs spyod rnams la stong pa snying
82a8(1): po nyid dang/lus las bla ma la snyen bkur bya ba'i snying po nyid dang/srog las dge ba'i rtsa ba sems skyed pa snying po nyid dang/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang cub sems dpa' rnaMs
82a9(1): kyis yongs su btang bar myi bya ba'o//bzhi gang zhe na/byang chub kyi sems yod ma su btang bar myi bya ba dang/dam pa'i chos yongs su btang bar spyi bya ba dang/sems can thams cad yongs su btang bar myi
82a10(1): bya ba dang/dge' pa'i rtsa ba'i chos thams cad yongs su btangabar myi bya ba ste/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag na/byang chub sems dpa' rnas kyi skyed mo 'tshal lo/bzhi gang zhe na/
82b1(1): dgon ba la gnas pa dang/dben ba la mngon bar dga' pa dang/dge' pa'i chos la 'dund pa dang/thabs mkhas pas sems can yongs su smyind bar byed pa ste/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di
82b2(1): dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi gzhal myed khang ngo//gzhi gang zhe na/tshangs ba'i gnas dang/'brel ba'i gtam gi chos mnyam pa la/dga' pa myong bar byed pa dang/stong ba nyid la nges par sems dpa' dang
82b3(1): skye bo'i rigs thun pa dang 'du ba ste/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni/byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi nor myi zad pa'o//bzhi gang zhe na/thos pa'i nor dang/chos stend ba'i nor dang/sems
82b4(1): can dbul po bdud ba'i nor dang/byang chub tu yongs su bsngo' ba'i nor ste/bzhi po de dag go/lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub ses dpa' rnams kyi gter ro//bzhi gang zhe na/gzungs kyi gter dang
82b5(1): sbobs pa'i gter dang/chos kyi gter dang/longs spyod zad myi shes pa'i gter dang/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dgani/byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams kyi mngon bar dga'
82b6(1): ba'o/bzhi gang zhe na/'du 'dzi las 'byung pa dang/yul thams cad las 'byung pa dang/'phags pa ma yin pa'i bsam ba las 'byung ba dang/khams sum thams cad las 'byung ba ste
82b7(1): bzhi po de dag ni mngon bar 'byung ba'o//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di da ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bde' ba'o//bzhi gang zhe na/dngos po thams cad las spyi lta ba'i phyir bdag gi myed
82b8(1): cing yongs su 'dzind pa myed pa'i bde' ba dang/yul spangs pas dben ba'i bde' ba'i bde' ba dang/nyon mongs pa myed pa'i phyir zhi ba'i bde ba dang/sems can thams cad yongs su myi gtong ba'i phyir/
82b9(1): mya ngan las 'das pa'i bde' ba ste/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa'i rnams kyi mchog tu dga' bo'//bzhi gang zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pa mthong bas
82b10(1): mchog tu dga' pa dang/chos thos pas mchog tu dga' ba dang/sbyin pa la 'byod pa myed pas mchog tu dga' pa dga' ba dang/sems can thamasacad la bde' ba skyed pas mchog tu dga' ba ste/bzhi bo de dag
83a1(1): //lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bden ba'o//bzhi gang zhe na/byang chub kyi sems myi gtong ba dang/yi dam las ma nyams pa dang/skyabs su song pa dag yongs
83a2(1): su myi gtong ba dang/ngag sdams pas tshig rnams bde ba ste/bzhi bo de dag go//lha'i bu dag bzhi bo 'di dag ni/byang tshub sems dpa' rnams kyi dge' ba'i chos/bzhi gang zhe na/dge' ba'i chos thams
83a3(1): cad la lhag ba'i bsam bas sbyor ba dang/ma slabs ba thams cad la myi rnyas ba dang/gsol ba btab par sems can thams cad la gshes gnyen du gyurd ba dang/yon tan thams cad sgrub pa la phyir bya ba'i re ba myed
83a4(1): pas lan la myi de ba ste/bzhi bo de dag ga//lha'i bu dag bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub semams dpa' rnams kyi spyod pa rnam par dag pa'o//bzhi gang zhe na/bdag myed pa'i tshul khrims rnam par dag ba dang/
83a5(1): sems can myed pa'i phyir/ting nge 'dzind rnam par dag pa dang/srog myed pa'i shes rab rnam par dag pa dang/zag pa myed pa'i phyir rnam par dag pa ste/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi
83a6(1): po 'di dag ni/byang chub sesam dpa' rnams kyi rkang pa'o/bzhi gang zhe na/chos kyi rkang pa dang/don kyi rkang pa dang/sbyangs pa'i yon tan dang/yo byad nyung bas 'jug pa'i rkang pa dang/
83a7(1): byang chub kyi lam kyi tshogs stsogs pa'i rkang pa ste/bzhi po de dag ga//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang cub sems dpa' rnams kyi lag pa'o//bzhi gang zhe na/dad pa'i lag pa dang/tshul khrims kyi
83a8(1): lag pa dang/thos pa'i lag pa dang/shes rab kyi lag pa ste/bzhi po de dag gi//lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi myig go/bzhi gang zhe na/las legs par byas pa'i
83a9(1): phyir sha'i myig dang/mngon par shes pa lnga las myi nyams pa'i phyir/lha'i myig dang/shind tu thos pa'i stobs dang ldan pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi myig dang/chos thams cad la nges sems pa'i phyir/chos kyi myig
83a10(1): ste/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dang ni/byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams kyi myi ngoms ba'o//dgon pa la gnas pas myi ngoms pa dang/dge' ba'i chos kyi tshogs thams cad kyis myi ngoms
83b1(1): pa dang/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dka' pa myed pa'o//bzhi gang zhe na/sems can mthu' chung pa rnams la bzod cing dang du blang pa dka' pa myed pa dang
83b2(1): dbul po rnams la dog pa myed pa dang/bdog pa thams cad yongs su gtang pa dang/bsam pa dga' pa myed pa dang/yan lag gi mchog mgo' slong ba la/khong khro ba myed pa'i sems dka' ba byed kyi/steng gi 
83b3(1): dge' ba'i shes gnyen du sems skyed pa dang/skye ba la myi rtog pas bsam bzhin du skye bar byed pa'i dka' ba byed pa ste/bzhi bo de dag go//lha'i bu 'di dag ni/byang chub sesam dpa' rnams kyi nad myed pa'o//
83b4(1): bzhi gang zhe na/khams mnyam bas nad myed pa dang/nyon mongs pa'i gdung ba myed pas nad myed pa dang/sems can thams cad bde' par 'jog pas nad myed pa dang/chos thams cad la the tsom myed pas nad myed
83b5(1): pa dang/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub/sems dpa' rnams kyi bdag gi phyogs so/bzhi gang zhe na/pha rold tu phyin pa'i phyogs dang/byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos kyi phyogs
83b6(1): dang/dge' pa'i shes gnyen yang dag pa'i phyogs dang/sdig pa thams cad myi bya ba'i phyogs dang/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dang ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi spyi 
83b7(1): skyong ba'o/bzhi gang zhe na/byang chub kyi sems myi skyod pa dang/yi dam myi skyod pa dang/ci skad smras pa de bzhin du byed pa las myi g.yo' ba dang/yang dag pa'i nan tan las myi skyod pa ste/bzhi 
83b8(1): po de dag go/lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag gi/byang chub sems dpa' rnalas kyi tshogs so//bzhi gang zhe na/zhi gnas kyi tshogs dang/lhag mthong gi tshogs dang/thos pa'i tshogs dang/dge' ba'i rtsa ba thams 
83b9(1): cad kyi tshogs ste/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa'i rnams kyi chos zung du 'brel par 'gyur ba'o//bzhi gang zhe na/bsam pa sbyor pa zung du 'brel pa dang/gtong 
83b10(1): ba dang sngo' ba zung du 'brel pa dang//byams pa dang/snying rje zung du 'brel ba dang/thabs dang/shes rab zung du 'brel pa dang /bzhi po de dag go/lha'i bu ba zhi po 'di dag ni/byang cub sems dpa'
84a1(1): //rnams kyi chos sgrib pa'i rgyu mthun pa'i rmi lam mo//bzhi gang zhe na/khron pa rdul can mthong zhing de'i gting na yang/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor mthong pa dang/mtsho' 'am/rdzing bu 'am/khron pa rnyog pa can
84a2(1): mthong zhing de'i gting na yar/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor mthor pa dang/par snang la srin chen bos g.yog cing de la yang zla ba'i dkyil 'khor mthong yang/bar snang la rlung dang/rdul dang/du bas khrigs bar mthong zhing de la yang
84a3(1): zla ba'i dkyil 'khor mthong ba dang/bzhi bo de dag go//lha'i pu bzhi bo 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi las kyi sgrib pa rgyu mthun ba'i rmi lam mo//bzhi gar zhe na/g.yang sa chen po nas bdag g.yar
84a4(1): sa chen por lhung pa mthong pa dang/lam thon man can du mthong pa dang/bdag lam 'phrang dog bar zhugs pa mthong pa dang/rmi lam na lhad cing 'jigs pa mang po mthong pa ste/bzhi bo de dag go/lha'i bu dag bzhi
84a5(1): po 'di dag ni/byang chub sesam dpa' rnams kyi nyon mongs ba'i sgrib ba'i rgyu mthun ba'i 'bras bu'o//bzhi gang zhe na/dug drag pos 'khrugs par mthong pa dang/gcan zan rtum
84a6(1): po phal po che'i sgra thos pa dang/bdag g.yon can gi nang na gnas bar mthong ba dang/bdag las kyi dri ma can du mthong pa dang//bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni//byang chub
84a7(1): sems dpa' rnams kyi gzungs thob pa'i rgyu thun pa'i rmi lam mo/bzhi gar zhe na/gter chen po rin po che sna tshogs kyis gang ba mthong pa dang/rdzing bu me tog bdams rgyas pas yongs su gang ba mthong pa
84a8(1): dang/bdag gos dkar po'i 'phrugs rnyed par mthong pa dang/gang zhig spyi bo'i gtsug na gdugs 'dzin bar mthong pa ste/bzhi po de dag go/lha'i bu dag gzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub 
84a9(1): sems dpa' rnams
84a10(1): kyi ting nge 'dzind thob pa'i rgyu mthun ba'i rmi lam mo//bzhi gang zhe na/bu mo bzang mo brgyand pa/me tog gis bsil ma dbul bar mthong pa dang/nam ka la bya dang ba'i rgyal po dkar po thal kar gyi khyu
84a11(1): rkang 'byind/'gro bar mthong ba dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od mang pos brgyan pa'i phyag spyi bor 'jog par 'dod pa dang/de bzhin gshegs me tog pad ma la bzhugs shing gtam mdzad bar mthong ba ste/
84b1(1): bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba'i rgyu mthun pa'i rmi lam mo/bzhi gang zhe na/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor char par mthong ba dang/nyi ma'i
84b2(1): dkyil 'khor char bar mthong ba dang/me tog pad ma kha 'bus par mthong ba dang/tshangs pa'i dbang po rab tu zhi ba'i spyod lam ste/lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni/byang chub sem dpa' rnams kyi de bzhinashegs
84b3(1): pa mthong ba'i rgyu thun ba'i rmi lam mo//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dgani/byang chub sems dpa' rarnams kyi/rang gi mtshan nyid rgyu mthun pa'i rmi lam lo//bzhi gang zhe na/shing sa la chen po 'dab sa dang
84b4(1): me tog dang/'bras bu sna tshogs kyis gang bar mthong ba dang/khang ba'i snod ser gis gang bar 'dod ba mthong pa dang/nam mkha'i dom gdugs dang/rgyal mtshan dang/ba dan gyis gang bar mthong ba dang
84b5(1): 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po chen po mthong ste/bzhi po de dag go/lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bdud btul ba'i rgyu thun pa'i rmi lam mo//
84b6(1): bzhi' gang zhe na/byang chen pos gyad thams cad mad nas pa dan thogs te 'gro par mthong pa dang/dpa' bo chen po g.yul las 'das te/'gro par mthong ba dang/rgyal pos dbang skur mthong
84b7(1): ba dang/bdag byang chub kyi snying po la 'dug cing 'dul bar mthong pa ste/bzhi po de dag go//lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni/byang chub sem dpa' rnams kyi/phyir myi ldog pa'i rtags kyi rgyu thun pa'i
84b8(1): rmi lam mo//bzhi gang zhe na/mgo' la thod kar pos bcings par mthong ba dang/bdag gtan pa myed pa'i chos sbyin byed par mthong pa dang/bdag chos stan chen po la 'dug par mthong pa dang/de bzhin
84b9(1): gshegs pa byang chub kyi snying po la bzhugs shing/chos ston par mthong pa ste/bzhi po de ngag go//lha'i bu bzhi po 'di dgani/byang chub sems dpa' rarnams kyi byang chub kyi snying po thob pa'i rgyu thun ba'i
84b10(1): rmi lam mo//bzhi gang zha na//bum pa mthong ba dang/bya tsha shas bskord par mthong pa dang/gang dang gang du 'gro ba de dang de nyid du shes shing/thams cad ldang zhing/bdud la phyag 'tshal bar byed pa dang
85a1(1): //gser gi kha dog lta bu snang ba chen po ma thob ste/lha'i bu bzhi bo 'di dag ni /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi /byang chub kyi snying bo la thob pa'i rgyu mthun pa'i rmi lam mo//
85a2(1): 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pas/chos bzhi sgrub pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs bshad pa na/lha'i bu dpal zangs ldan dga' mgu' yid rangs te/rab tu dga' zhing dga' ba dang/yid bde' pa skyes nas
85a3(1): 'khor dang bcas pas/'jam dpal gzho nur gyungad pa la/mchod pa'i las bya ba'i phyir/lha'i me tog man d+ha ra ba dag dang/ub pa la dang/pad ma dang/ku mu ta dang/bad ma kar po dag 
85a4(1): gyis /thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor la gtor to//me tog de dag gtor ma thag tu /des de'i tshe /sangs rgyas kyi mthus steng gi bar snang la/me tog gi shing pad ma'i shing rta'i 'phang
85a5(1): lo tsam gyi tshad lta na sdug pa/gsung dang ldan pa /yid du 'ong ba dang ldan bar gyurd do//me tog de dag dbus kyi snying po la/byang chub sems dpa' lus skyes bu chen po'i 
85a6(1): mtshan sum bcu rtsa nyis kyis brgyand pa dag kyang 'byung par gyurd to//de nas 'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd pa la/lha'i bu dpal zangs ldan gis 'di skad smras pa/'jam dpal byang 
85a7(1): chub sems dpa' 'di dag gang nas glags/'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i me tog 'di dag nyid la gnas so//lha'i bus smras pa/'jam dpal me tog 'di dag ni sprul pa ste/bdag gyis
85a8(1): khyod la mchod pa'i las bgyi pa'i slad du gtor to//'jam dpal gis smras ba/lha'i bu ci ltar me tog de dag sprul pa yin pa ltar/byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi lus la yang de bzhin du lta'o//
85a9(1): de nas de'i tshe /bcom ldan 'das 'dzum pa mdzad do //'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams gang gi tshe/'dzum pa mdzad pa'i chos nyid de de'i tshe/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal gi sgo nas/
85a10(1): 'od zer kha dog sna tshogs/kha dog du ma 'di lta ste/sngon po dang/gser po dang/dmar po dang/dkar po dang/leb rgan dang/shel gi kha dog lta bu dag 'byung ngo//de dag gyis kyang
85b1(1): 'jig rten gi khams mtha' yas mu myed pas/snang bas khyab par byas nas/steng du tshangs pa'i 'jig rten gi bar du mngon par byung ste/gza'i brjid kyis/nyi ma dang/zla ba dang/gza'i rjid kyis zil 
85b2(1): kyis gnan par slar log ste/bcom ldan 'das kyi dbu'i btsug tu nub ste/de nas lha'i bu dpal zangs ldan stan las yangs te/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas/bus mo g.yas pa'i lha ngas la btsugs ste/
85b3(1): bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su gcad pas stod pa//gser bzang dri ma myed pa'i gzi' brjid ldan//skye mched mchog mtshan sum bcu pa nyis
85b4(1): sna tshogs mang//yon tan khrag khrig yon tan mtha' yas gzi'i brjid can//mgon po ci'i slad du 'dzum pa stond par mdzad//sgra snyan 'byin par mdzad cing rab tu gsung 'phel ba//tshangs pa'i 
85b5(1): dbyangs su gsung zhing 'jam pa'i tshig gsung pa//nor bu 'od zer rgya chen bde' gshegs blo gros ldan//ka la bying ka'i gsung can ci slad 'dzum pa gsungs//myi mchog skyes bu dam pa ngan pa'i 
85b6(1): tshogs can 'dul//bdud kyi stobs ni rtag par ma thar 'byind par mdzad//lha dang lha myin nam mkha' lding gis rtag mchod pa//stobs bcu ldan pas ci'i slad du 'dzum ba 
85b7(1): mdzad//dri myed dri bral dri ma gsung spangs pa//zla ba nya pa'i dkyil 'khor lta bur zhal rgyas pa//mun pa rab rib sgrib pa'i rdul gi nyes gcod pa//de dang de ldan sbyin par mdzad 
85b8(1): pa'i 'dzum don gsungs//bde bar gshegs pa sa dang lha yul phan pa mchog//de dang de ldan sbyin mdzad yon tan mtha' yas pa//mtshungs shing 'breld pa legs sungs gsal ba'i gsung ldan pa//
85b9(1): 'dzum ba mdzad ba 'di'i don ni ci ltar rtogs par bgyi'//ling tog stug po'i mun pa rab tu rnam gsal pa//shes rab sgron ma 'od kyi gzi' brjid ldan pa po//glang chen khyu mchog stabs dang/seng ge'i 
85b10(1): stabs ltar bgyid//'gro ba'i don mdzad 'dzum pa'i don mdzad don ni stand du gsol//myi'i dam pa mya ngan chos can 'dul mdzad pa//zab cing mthong dka' gting tshod dpag dka' ba//srid pa'i chu 
86a1(1): /ni skems mdzad mnyam pa can//rgyu myed stobs bcu ldan gis 'dzum pa ci slad stand//rga shi zhi bar bgyi pa'i 'dud rtsi'i don gsal ba//zhabs ni 'jam zhing dra ba dag dang
86a2(1): 'khor lor ldan//blan ba sbyong mdzad srid pa gsum na mnyam byed pa//ye shes chen po mnga' pas 'dzum don stan du gsol//de skad ces bka' stsald pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis/lha'i bu
86a3(1): dpal zangs ldan la 'di skad ces bka' stsald ba/lha'i bu khyod kyis byang chub sems dpa' steng gi par snang la/seng ge'i khri pad ma'i snying po la bkod pa 'di dag mthong ngam/gsold ba/bcom ldan 'das
86a4(1): mthong lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa//lha'i bu byang chub sems dpa' phyogs bcu nas/'dus pa 'di dag thams cad ni/'jam dpal gzhonur gyurd pa'i drung du/chos nyan
86a5(1): pa dang/bzhi ba sgrub pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di nyan pa'i phyir lhags so//yang byang chub sems dpa' 'di dag/thams cad ni/'jam dpalagazhonur gyurd bas/yongs su smin bar byas
86a6(1): pa ma yin no//yang byang chub sems pa' 'di dag thams cad ni /bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skyed pa gcig gis thogs pa ste/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gi
86a7(1): khams mying sna tshogs kyi /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan dang/gzhan dag tu bla na myed pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i byang chub du /mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur ro//lha'i bus gsold ba//bcom
86a8(1): ldan 'das bdag gis grangs gtogs par rngo myi thog ni ci snyed cig mchis//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis //tshe dang ldan pa sha ra'i bu la bka' stsald ba/sha ra'i bu khyod kyis byang chub sems dpa'
86a9(1): 'di dag gi grangs gtogs bar nus bsam/gsold pa/bcom ldan 'das bdag gis/stong sum gi stod chen po 'jig rten gi khams na/gang dang skar ma'i gzungs de dag thams cad ni/skad cig/thang cig
86a10(1): phung tsam de la cig gis bgrang bar spro bar gyurd kyis/bcom ldan 'das bdagagasi/byang chub sems dpa' 'di dag ni/lo rgyang yang bgrang bar spro ba ma lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba
86b1(1): sha ri'i bu gal te 'dzam bu'i gling 'di rdul phra rab tsam gi /rdul gis gang bar 'gyur gis /rdul phra rab bgrang pa'i tshul gis mtha' rtogs par nus kyi /byang chub sems dpa' 'di dag ni bgrang ba'i tshul gis 
86b2(1): mtha' rtogs par myi nus so//byang chub sems dpa' 'dus pa 'di dag ni de tsam du tshad myed do//gsold pa /bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' 'di ngag gang du /byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 
86b3(1): 'tshang rgya pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ci snyed mchis par 'gyur //bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' sa la dab/sha ri'i bu kha rog de skad ma smra cig/sha ri'i bu sangs rgyas kyi zhing tshad myed ba dag /de bzhin gshegs 
86b4(1): pa stong ngo //sha ri'i bu /yongs su rtag pa bzung na/gal te de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshe'i tshad /skald pa gang 'ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed du gyurd na/nyi ma re re thams cad la yang chos bshad pa /gang 'ga'i 
86b5(1): klung gi bye ma snyed du 'jug par gyurd cing /chos bshad pa thams cad la yang /byang chub sems dpa' gang 'ga' klung gi bye ma snyed lung stan par gyurd la/nyi ma re re thams cad pa la yang chos bshad ba /gang 'ga'i 
86b6(1): klung gyi bye ma snyed du 'jug bar gyurd cing /chos bshadapa thams cad la yang //byang chub sems dpa' gang dga' klung gi bye ma snyed lungastan par gyurd la/gang gyis shar phyogs su sangs rgyas kyi zhing
86b7(1): gang 'ga'i klung bye ma snyed 'das nas /byang chub sems dang 'a cig mtshan mtshan mar bzhag kyang /sangs rgyas zhing rnams de bzhin gshegs pa dag gis de tsam du stong na/phyogs bcu'i 
86b8(1): sangs rgyas kyi zhing gang de bzhin gshegs pa'i /sha'i spyan tha mal pas gzigs pas gyurd pa'i /sangs rgyas kyi zhing de dag tu skyes pa'i sems can de dag /sems /de bzhin gshegs pa dag gi de tsam du 
86b9(1): de dag nad pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gis stong pa lta ci smos//de nas nyan thos chen po de dag dang /thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de ngo mtshar du gyurd nas/tshig 'di skad du /bdag cag gi 
86b10(1): bstond pa de dag de ltar rdzu 'phrul che/de ltar mthu' che/de ltar ye shes gzigs pa chen po dang ldan te/bdag cag gis rnyed pa legs par snyed do//zhes smras so//de nas byang chub 
87a1(1): //sems dpa' gang phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gi khams nas/'dus pa steng gi par snang la 'khod pa de dag/bar snang de las babs nas/bcom ldan 'das dang/'jam dpal gzho nur gyurd 
87a2(1): ka pa'i zhabs la mgo' bos phyag 'tshal te/skor nas phyogs bcur dong ngo//de nas 'jam dpal gzho cung gyurd pa la/lha'i bu dpal zangs ldan gis 'di skad ces smras so//'jam dpal khyod kyis
87a3(1): sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa mdzad nas /sems can tshad myed grangs myed pa /byang chub tu yongs su smind bar mdzad pa /legs so//'jam dpal khyo kyis /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi /byang
87a4(1): chub yongs su smind par bgyid pa'i chos las rtsams te/spong par mdzod cig/'jam dpal gis smras pa/lha'i bu byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyi /byang chub yongs su smind bar byed pa'i
87a5(1): chos ni sum bcu rtsa lnga'o//gsum bcu rtsa lnga gar zhe na/'di lta ste dus su skul ba dang/tshod la skul ba dang/ran pa na skul ba dang/dpang po la skul ba dang/brtan bar skul ba dang/
87a6(1): pha rold tu phyind pa la skul ba dang/thabs la skul pa dang/lhag ba'i bsam pa la skul ba dang/byams pa chen po la mngon bar skul ba dang/snying rje chen po la bar skul ba dang
87a7(1): theg pa chen po la skul pa dang/theg pa dman ba la skul ba dang/bden ba la skul ba dang/de bya ba la skul ba dang/chos srung par skul ba dang/thos pa rtan par skul ba dang/sems can sna
87a8(1): tshogs su dbye' bar skul ba dang/tshul khrims dang ldan bar skul ba dang/tshul khri mas dang ldan ba la sbyin pa'i gnas su mtshungs par skul bar dang/bdud kyi las bstand bar skul ba dang/dam bcad
87a9(1): ba nyams 'og tu chud par skul ba dang/'khor bas yongs su myi skyo bar skul ba dang/bdud btul bar skul ba dang/byas ba shes ba dang/byas pa bzo' bar skul ba dang/rgyu zad ba la 'jug bar skul

87b1(1): 'jig rten gi chos dang ma 'dres par skul pa dang/dgon pa la dga' bar skul pa dang/'dod pa chung zhing mchog shes par skul pa dang/'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/ma grol pha grol ba dang/dbugs ma

87b3(1): pa yongs su dag pa blang par skul ba ste/lha'i bu byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi/byang chub yongs su smyind par byed pa'i/chos gsum bcu' rtsa lnga de dag yin no//lha'i bu de ltar byang

88a2(1): bar mngon no//gang na chos kyi rnam grangs 'di spyod par gyurd pa de ni/de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi bdag nyid brtson par mdzad do//gang chos kyi rnam grangs 'di la nyan par byed pa'i sems can de dag
88a3(1): ni nga'i yin bar 'gyur ro//gang dag chos kyi rnam grangs 'di mnyand pa dang/mos pa'i sems can 'di dag ni/de bzhin gshegs bas 'dul par dka'o//gang dag chos kyi rnam drangs 'di 'dzind pa

92a2(1): sde dang/lha'i bu brgyan ljon pa dang/spos kyi glang po che dang/bden pa po dang/ye shes tog dang/chos kyi tog dang/zla ba tog dang/nyi ma'i tog dang/mtha' yas tog dang/tshig myed pa dang/

92b1(1): da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dang/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis skyed pa dang/skyed par 'gyur pa dang/skyed pa de ltar bdag gyis kyang skyed par bgyi' ste/bsam pha dang/lhag pa'i bsam
92b2(1): pa'i rgyud des de ltar bskyed par bgyi'o//gang 'gog pa ma mchis pa dang/dpag pa ma mchis pa dang/bas chags pa ma mchis pa dang/sgrib pa ma mchis pa dang/rtogs pa ma mchis pa dang /rnam par rtog pa
92b3(1): ma mchis pa dang/de ltar skyed par bgyi'o//yang na lus ma mchis pa dang/yang na lus la gdags pa ma mchis pa dang/ngag ma mchis pa dang/ngag tu gdags pa ma mchis pa dag/sems ma mchis pa dang/

93b4(1): pa rnams dang/phyir myi ldog pa rnams dang/skye ba cig gis thogs pa rnams dang/sa bcur zhugs pa rnams dang /dbang ma skur pa rnams dang/dbang skur ba thob pa rnams dang/dbang skur

112a8(1): drin lan bsab pa'i mdo' dam pa/nyan cing mchi' bar byin kyis rlab tu gsol//de nas sangs rgyas des/byang chub sems dpa' de rnams la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu dag khyed myi mjed kyi 
112a9(1): 'jig rten gi khams 'dir phyind na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de la mchod cing kur sti bya ba dang /shind tu phrad dka' pa'i 'du shes skyed par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/sangs rgyas shag kya thub pa ni/skald pa 
112a10(1): grangs myed pa bye ba khrag khrig gyi bar du shind tu spyod do//snying rje chen po'i smon lam 'di skad ces btab po/gal te mngon par sangs rgyas pa na/'jig rten gi khams sdig pa can/ri dang /shar shong dang /gyo mo dang /
112b1(1): seg ma dang/tsher ma las stsogs pa yod pa'i gnas su /sems can dag kyang nyon mongs pa dang/bcas pa /mtshams myed pa lnga dang/myi dge' ba bcu rdzogs par byed pa'i gnas der mngon par sangs rgyas te/phan pa dang/bde
112b2(1): ba'i don tu sdug sngal thams cad yongs su gcad de /bde ba thams cad dang ldan par byas nas/chos kyi sku yongs su rdzogs shing /lhag ma ma lus pa'i mya ngan las 'das par gyurd cig ces smon lam btab bo //sangs
112b3(1): rgyas bcom ldan 'das des /thog ma de skad ces smon lam btab pas/khyed rnams 'jig rten gi khams der phyind nas/ci ltar sangs rgyas nyid gshegs pa dang/ci ltar sangs rgyas nyid bzhugs pa dang 'dra bar gnas par
112b4(1): bya'o zhes bka' stsald pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad kyis /tshig thun par bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' bzhin du bgyi'o zhes sol nas/byang chub sems dpa' re re yang 'khor /byang chub sems dpa'
112b5(1): bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong snyed kyis yongs su skor te/myi mjed kyi 'jig rten gi khams gang na yod pa der song ngo//de dag 'jig rten gi khams gangadang gang dag tu phyind par /'jig
112b6(1): rten gi khams de dag rnam pa drug tu g.yos te/'od zer chen po yang kun tu snang par gyurd to/steng gi par snang las lha rnams kyis /me tog man d+ha ra pa dang/man d+ha
112b7(1): ra ba chen po 'tshang zhing 'bab bo /'od zer chen pos snang par byas shing rdzu 'phrul gis /'jig rten gi khams gang
112b8(1): de ma yin pa'i gzhan yang lha rdzas kyi rol mo tshad myed pa /rgya stong snyed kyang steng gi bar snang la ma bkrol par rang zer bzhin du /byang chub sems dpa' de dag bya rgod spungs pa'i ri la /de bzhin shegs
112b9(1): pa'i spyan sngar lhags nas /zhabs la mgo' bos phyag tshal te /sangs rgyas la lan sum skor ba byas nas /phyogs cig tu 'khod do//de'i tshe de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od zer gyis /
112b10(1): lho phyogs logs su sangs rgyas kyi zhing phye ba khrag khrig brgya bcu 'das pa'i pha rold na/'jig rten gi khams yon tan 'od ces bya bar snang par byas so//'jig rten gi khams de na sangs rgyas
113a1(1): //mtshan ma dgongs shes bya ba /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas /rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa/bdel bar gshegs pa /'jig rten mkhyen pa
113a2(1): skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba/bla na myed pa/stond pa rgyal ba/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi /sangs rgyas kyi zhing shind tu dga' ba zhes bya ba na zhugs te/sangs rgyas kyi zhing de yang
113a3(1): shind tu mnyam zhing bzhi bye ru t+ya las byas pa/gser tso mas thig re 'ur pris pa/rin po che sna bdun gyi shing skyes pa /shing de dag kyang mda' rgyang gang tsam du mthong /me tog dang/'bras bu dang/
113a4(1): yal ga dang/'dab mas rgyan pa/rlung drag pos skyod ba /sgra snyand pa 'byung pa /sgra de dag so so'i skye pos thos par myi ngoms pa/skabs skabs na chu myig dang/mtshe'u dang/khrus kyi rdzing
113a5(1): bu yod pa /yon tan brgyad dang ldan pa'i chus yongs su gang pa /rdzing gyi zhabs kyang gser gi bye ma brdal ba/rdzing bu'i ngos bzhi las /gsung dam pa dang ldan pa'i me tog dam pa dang/bun da
113a6(1): ri ka dang/pa ri sha ka dang/de ma yin pa'i me tog sngon pho dang/ser po dang/dmar po dang/dkar po dang/shing rta'i phang lo tsam du chu'i steng yongs su khebs te
113a7(1): chu de'i nang na bya rnam pa sna tshogs kyang /shind tu yid du 'ong ba'i sgra snyand pa 'byind to//rdzing de'i nang na rin po che sna bdun las byas pa'i gru yod de/de na sems can mang
113a8(1): po 'dod dgur rtse zhing longs spyod do//rin po che'i shing de dag gi bar bar na/seng ge'i khri mthong khyad dpag tshad cig pa bshams nas /rin po che sna bdun gis rgyan te/lha rdzas kyi na bza'
113a9(1): khri'i steng du rim par bting ba/lha rdzas kyi spos kyi dri thul pa /lha rdzas kyi me tog gis chal par bkram pa'i khri la /de bzhin gshegs pa mtshan ma dgongs pa dkyil mo krung bcas shing bzhugs pa 
113a10(1): byang chub sems dpa' tshad myed pa /bye ba rgya stod snyed mdun tu bdar te/thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/de bzhin gshegs pa de la so so nas /tshig thun bar 'di skad ces bsol to//bcom ldan 'das
113b1(1): ci'i rgyu ci'i rkyen kyis 'ong bzang po /kha dog sngon po dang/dmar po dang/dkar por ldan pa/rab tu gsal pa /dper myi bzod pa 'di /byang phyogs logs nas 'khor mang po 'di dag la snang
113b2(1): par gyur te/gang la 'od kyis reg pa de dag thams cad /lus dang sems pa de' bar gyurd pa/thugs rtse ba'i slad du/bcom ldan 'das kyis stan du gsol //de la bcom ldan 'das
113b3(1): nges 'di skad ces bka' stsald to//rigs kyi bu dag legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang/khyed la bkrol zhing bshad par bya'o//'di nas byang phyogs logs kyi 'jig rtend gi khams bye ba
113b4(1): khrag khrig brgya bcu 'das ba'i pha rol na/sangs rgyas kyi zhing myi mjed zhes bya ba yod de /de na sangs rgyas shag kya thub pa zhes bya ba /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang
113b5(1): dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas /rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa /bde' bar gshegs pa /'jig rten mkhyend pa/skyes bu 'dul pa'i kha lo sgyur ba/bla na myed pa/
113b6(1): ston pa rgyal ba/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba 'khor mang pos yongs su skor nas/'khor mang po thams cad/thabs la mkhas pa chen po drin lan bsab pa'i mdo' sde
113b7(1): ston cing /sems can thams cad la phan pa dang/bde' ba'i don kyi phyir sems can thams cad kyi log par lta ba'i zug rngu rtsa nas 'don pa'i phyir/sems can thog ma pa'i byang chub sems dpa'
113b8(1): dag/byang chub las phyir myi ldog cing rtand par bya ba'i phyir /nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas thams cad mthar phyind pa'i /theg pa cig gis lam la 'god pa'i phyir /byang chub sems dpa'
113b9(1): chen po rnams /myur tu mngon par byang chub cing/sangs rgyas kyi drin lan bsab pa'i phyir /sems can thams cad kyi drin lci pa dran par bya pa'i phyir /sems can thams cang sdug sngal gi rgya mtsho' las
113b10(1): sgrol ba'i phyir /so so'i skye bo dag pha ma la sri zhu bya bar 'dod pa'i phyir /'od 'di snang bar gyurd pa yin no//de'i tshe 'khor de'i nang na/byang chub sems dpa' 'khor mang po'i gtso bo dang/
114a1(1): //stond par gyurd pa /stong phrag bcu snyed stan las langs te/bla gos phyogs cig tu byas nas /pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to
114a2(1): bcom ldan 'das kyi byin kyi rlabs kyis bdag cag rnams /myi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams der mchi' zhing /de bzhin gshegs pa shag kya thub pa la mchod pa dang/snyen bkur bgyi ba dang/thabs la mkhas ba

114a6(1): na /'jig rten gi khams sdig pa can /ri dang/gyo mo dang shang shong dang/seg ma dang/tsher ma la sogs pa yod pa'i gnas su /sems can dag kyang nyon mongs pa dang bcas pa dang

114a9(1): 'jig rten gi khams der phyind nam/ci ltar sangs rgyas nyid gshegs pa dang/ci ltar sangs rgyas nyid bzhugs pa dang/'dra par bya'o zhes bka' stsald pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad
114a10(1): tshig thun par bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' bzhin du bgyi'o zhes gsol nas /byang chub sems dpa' dedag re re yang /'khor byang chub sems dpa' bye pa khrag khrig rgya' stong snyed kyis yongs su skord /myi mjed 
114b1(1): kyi 'jig rten gi khams ga na ba der song ngo /de dag 'jig rten gi khams gang dang gang du phyind par /'jig rten gi khams de dag rnam pa drug tu g.yos te/'od zer chen pos kun tu snang bar gyurd/steng gi par 
114b2(1): snang las lha rnams kyis /me tog man d+ha ra ba dang /man da ra ba chen po yang char bzhin tu 'bab bo//'od zer chen pos snang par byas shing /rdzu 'phrul gis 'jig rten gi khams gang 'ga' klung gyi bye ma

114b4(1): la ba der /de bzhin gshegs pa de'i spyan sngar lhags nas/zhabs la mgo' bos phyag 'tshal te/sangs rgyas la lan gsum skor pa byas nas/phyogs cig tu 'khod do //de'i tshe de bzhin gshegs
114b5(1): pa'i 'od zer gis /nub phyogs logs su sangs rgyas kyi zhing tshad myed pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong 'das ba'i pha rol na/'jig rten gi khams dag pa'i gnas zhes bya ba snang bar byas so//
114b6(1): 'jig rten gi khams de na/sangs rgyas kyi 'tshan nyi zla mar me'od ces bya ba /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas /rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa bde bar
114b7(1): gshegs pa /'jig rten mkhyend pa /skyes bu 'dul pa'i kha lo sgyur ba /bla na myed pa/stond pa rgyal ba /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das /sangs rgyas kyi zhing bde' ba can zhes bya

114b9(1): du mtho' ba/me tog dang/'bras bu dang/yal ga dang/'dab ma shind tu rgyan pa/rlung myi drag pas skyong na sgra snyan pa 'byung pa /sgra de dag so so'i skye bos thos pas myi ngoms pa/skabs skabs na chu 
114b10(1): myig dang/mtshe'u dang/'khrus kyi rdzing bu yod la /yon tan brgyad dang ldan pa'i chus yongs su gang zhing/rdzing gi zhabs kyang sger gi phye mas brdal pa/rdzing gyi ngos bzhi na gsung dam pa dang ldan pa'i me tog
115a1(1): ///pad ma dang/pun da ri ka dang/pa ri sha ka dang/de ma yin pa'i me tog sngon po dang/gser po dang/dmar po dang/dkar po shing rta'i phang lo tsam chu'i steng yongs su khebs te/

115a4(1): tog gyis cha lad bar bkram pa'i khri de la/de bzhin gshegs pa nyi zla mar me'i 'od dkyil krung bcas shing bzhugs pa la/byang chub sems dpa' tshad myed pa bye ba rgya stong snyed mdun tu bdar te/thal mo sbyar ba/
115a5(1): btud nas/de bzhin gshegs pa de la so so nas tshig thun par 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ci'i rgyu ci'i rkyen 'od bzang bo kha dog sngon po dang/gser po dang/dmar po dang
115a6(1): dkar por ldan pa rab tu gsal ba/dper myi bzod pa/'di shar phyogs logs nas 'khor mang po 'di dag la snang bar gyurd to/gang la 'od kyis reg pa de dag thams cad lus dang/sems 
115a7(1): bde bar gyurd/thugs rtse ba'i slad du bcom ldan 'das kyis stan du gsol/de la bcom ldan 'das des 'di skad ces bka' stsald to//rigs kyi bu dag legs par nyon la yid la zung shig
115a8(1): dang/khyed la dkrol zhing bshad par bya'o//'di nas shar phyogs logs su 'jig rten gi khams tshad myed pa bye ba khrag khrig 'das pa'i pha rol na/sangs rgyas kyi zhing myi mjed ces bya ba yod de/de na sangs
115a9(1): rgyas shag kya thub ba zhes bya ba/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas/rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa bde bar gshegs pa/'jig rtend mkhyend pa/skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo
115a10(1): sgyur ba bla na myed pa/ston pa rgyal pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba/'khor mang pos yongs su bskord nas/'khor mang po thams cad la/thabs mkhas pa chen po drin lan bsaba'i
115b1(1): mdo' sde stond cing/sems can thams cad la phan ba dang/bde' pa'i don kyi phyir sems can thams cad kyi log par lta ba'i zug rngu rtsa nas 'don pa'i phyir/sems thog ma ba'i byang chub sems dpa' dag/

116a8(1): phyag 'tshal te/sangs rgyas la lan gsum skor pa byas nas /phyogs cig tu 'khod do/de'i tshe de bzhin gshegs pas shag kya thub pas/kha dog rnam pa lnga dang ldan pa'i 'od zer byang phyogs su /

191a3(1): sangs rgyas shag kya thub pa la skyabs su gsol lam/'on te dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas su gsol/bka' stsald pa/dus gsum gi sangs rgyas la skyabs su gsalo//ci lta zhes na/sangs
191a4(1): rgyas kyi chos ni sku cig tu bun pa'i phyir ro//sangs rgyas gcig la skyabs su song ba nyid/sangs rgyas la skyabs so sor ste/sangs rgyas tha dad pa myed pa'i phyir ro/yang gzhan du na dus gsum
191a5(1): gyi sangs rgyas la skyabs su sol lo zhes bgyi' ba lha rnams kyis/kha cig ni sangs rgyas 'or srung gi nye gnas yin par smra/kha cig ni sangs rgyas rnam par bzigs kyi nye gnas
191a6(1): yin zhes smra ste/de bzhin du sangs rgyas bdun car la yang so so nas bdag ni sangs rgyas che ge mo zhig gi nye gnas yin zhes smra pas/de bas na sangs rgyas cig la skyabs su gsol
191a7(1): par 'os kyi/dus gsum myi rigs so/gzhan yang de ltar myi rung ngo zhes smra pa yang yod do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/dper na rnam thos gyi bus dris pa'i mdo' las/rgyal po rnam thos kyi
191a8(1): bu dkon mchog gsum la skyabs su gsol pa ni/'das pa dang/sa byon pa dang/da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas la skyabs su gsol lo zhes gsungs te/don de dang sbyar na dus gsum gi
191a9(1): sangs rgyas rnams la skyabs su gsol par mngon no//gsold pa/gal te de lta bu lags na/lha'i bu dag sa so so nas bdag ni/sangs rgyas che ge mo zhes bya ba'i nye gnas lags so/
191a10(1): zhes bgyi' pa'i don ci lta bu lags/bka' stsald pa/lha'i bus smras pa la don nyes par 'chad pa yang ci yod//lha'i bu kha cig ni sangs rgyas gcig la skyabs su 'gro pa yang yod/
191b1(1): kha cig ni dus sum gi sangs rgyas la skyabs su song ba yang yod de/mdor na sangs rgyas cig la bya'o//gsold pa/ci la skyabs su mchi' na /sangs rgyas la skyabs su gsol ba zhes bgyi//
191b2(1): bka' stsald pa/skyabs su 'gro pa zhes bya ba ni ngag gi 'khor lo ste /thams cad mkhyend pa slob pa myed pa'i yon tan la skyabs su song pa 'o//gsold pa /bzugs kyi sku la skyabs su mchi' 'am
191b3(1): chos kyi sku la skyabs su mchi'o /bka' stsald pa /chos kyi sku la skyabs su 'gro'i/bzugs kyi sku la skyabs du 'gro ba ma yin te /bzugs sangs rgyas ma yin pa'i phyir ro//gsold pa/
191b4(1): bzugs kyi sku sangs rgyas ma lags na /ci'i slad du sangs rgyas kyi sku la khrag phyung bas /'tshams ma mchis pa'i las bgyis par 'gyur/bka' stsald pa/bzugs kyi sku ni chos kyi sku'i
191b5(1): snod yin te/chos kyi sku'i gnas yin no/de bas na bzugs kyi sku la gnod pa byas pas 'tshams myed pa'i las su 'gyur gyi /bzugs kyi sku sangs rgyas yin pas /'tshams
191b6(1): myed pa'i las su 'gyur ba ni ma yin no//gsold pa/ci la skyabs su mchi' na /chos la skyabs su mchi' zhes bgyi'/bka' stsald pa /skyabs su 'gro ba zhes bya
191b7(1): pa ni ngag gi 'khor lo ste /'dod pa dang 'dod pa myed pa'i mthar phyind cing /mya ngan las 'das par skyabs su song ba ni chos la skyabs su song ba zhes bya'o//gsold pa /bdag gyi
191b8(1): lus mthard phyind pa la skyabs su mchi' 'am/gzhan gi lus la mthar phyind pa la skyabs su mtshi' /bka' stsald pa/bdag gi lus mthar phyind pa dang/bzhan gi lus thar phyind pa la
191b9(1): skyabs su 'gro ba ni/chos la skyabs su song ba zhe bya'o//gsold pa/ci ltar na dge' 'dun la skyabs su mchi' ba lags /bka' stsald pa /skyabs

193a1(1): //skyabs su mchi' 'am/don dam pa'i dge' 'dun la skyabs su mchi'/gal te don dam pa'i dge' 'dun skyabs su 'gro na ni/sngon sangs rgyas kyis de 'dra dang ba'i ri la skyabs su 'gro ba gsum
193a2(1): 'phog ba'i tshe/phyis dge' 'dun zhes bya ba yod par 'gyur gis/de la skyabs su song shig ces gsungs pa myi rigs te/don dam pa'i dge' 'du la rtag tu 'jig rten na mchis pa lags/bka' stsald pa/
193a3(1): kun rdzob kyi bden pa'i dge' 'dun ni/don dam pa'i dge' 'dus gi rten pa'i gnas yin pas na/phyis dge' 'dun gis yod par 'gyur gyis/de la skyabs su song zhig ces gsungs pa yin no//yang gzhan
193a4(1): du na kun rdzob kyi bden pa'i dga' 'dun la bkur zhing btsun par bya ba'i phyir de skad ces gsungs te/sangs rgyas kyis ni 'khor thams cad kyi nang na sangs rgyas kyi 'khor mchog go//dper na 'o ma la zho 'byung la
193a5(1): zho las par 'byung/mar las mar gi snying po 'byung ste/mar gi snying po ni mchog rab gya nom pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi 'khor dge' 'dun yang de dang 'dra ste/nye gnas dge' 'dun yod na
193a6(1): de'i nang na 'jug pa bzhi dang/'bras bu bzhi thob pa bla na myed pa'i bsod nams kyi zhing yod bas na/'khor rnam pa dgu' bcu rtsa drug gi nang na/dge' 'dun ni gya nom pa mchog rab dpe'
193a7(1): zla myed pa ste/de bas na phyis yod par 'gyur gyis/skyabs su song shig ces gsungs pa la don dang 'gal ba myed do//gsold pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos lags la/chos nyid kyang dge' 'dun lags te/
193a8(1): dge' 'dun nyid kyang chos lags pas/yang dag par na chos cig tu bas na khyad par dam bye brag ci mchis/bka' stsald pa/chos de dag cig mod kyi don kyi sgo nas bye brag yod do//dkon mchog gsum zhes bya ba bstan
193a9(1): myed par ye shes chen po dang/slob pa myed pa'i sa bon te/yon tan thams cad dang ldan pa ni/sangs rgyas dkon mchog ces bya'o//'gog ba'i bden ba dang/'dus ma byas pa ni chos dkon mchog ces bya'o//
193a10(1): nyan thos kyi slob pa dang/myi slob pa'i yon tan dang/shes rab tu ldan pa ni dge' 'dun dkon mchog ces bya'o/chos kyi sgo nas dbye' na stan pa byed pa dang/slab pa myed pa'i chos ni sangs rgyas dkon mchog cesabya'o//
193b1(1): 'gog pa'i bden pa dang/'dus ma byas pa dang/slob pa yang ma yin no//myi slob pa yang ma yin pa'i chos dkon mchog ces bya'o//nyan thos kyi slob pa dang/myi slob pa'i chos ni dge' 'dun dkon
193b2(1): mchog ces bya'o//dbang po'i sgo nas dbye' ba sangs rgyas ni kun shes pa'i dbang po'o//chos ni dbang po'i chos ma yin no/dge' 'dun ni zag pa myed pa'i dbang po'o/bden pa'i sgo nas dbye' na/
193b3(1): sangs rgyas ni lam gi bden pa'i cha cig go//chos ni 'gog pa'i bden pa'o/dge' 'dun yang lam gi bden pa'i phyogs cha cig go//dge' sbyong gi 'bras bu dbye na/sangs rgyas ni dge' sbyor ro/
193b4(1): chos ni dge' sbyong gi 'bras bu'o/dge' 'dun ni dge sbyong ngo/chos dkon mchog ni dge' 'byong gi 'bras bu'o/bram ze'i sgo nas dbye' na /sangs rgyas ni bram ze'o//chos ni bram ze'i 'bras bu'o//
193b5(1): dge' 'dun ni bram ze'o/chos dkon mchog ni bram ze'i 'bras bu'o//tshangs par spyod pa'i sgo nas dbye' na/sangs rgyas ni tshangs par spyod pa'o/chos ni tshangs par spyod pa'i 'bras bu'o//dge' 'dun
193b6(1): ni tshangs par spyod pa'o//chos dkon mchog ni tshangs par spyod pa'i 'apras bu'o/rgyu dang 'bras bu'i sgo nas dbye na sangs rgyas ni rgyu'o//chos ni 'bras bu'o/dge' 'dun na rgyu'o/
193b7(1): lam dang 'bras bu'i sgo nas dbyi na/sangs rgyas ni lam mo chos ni 'bras bu'o/dge 'dun ni lam mo/chos dkon mchog ni 'bras bu'o//sangs rgyas kyi stond pa ni chos yin te/chos
193b8(1): las sangs rgyas skyes pa'i chos ni sangs rgyas kyi yum ste/sangs rgyas ni chos la gnas so//gsold pa/sangs rgyas kyi stond pa chos lags na/ci'i slad du dkon mchog gsum la chos sngar myi smos/
193b9(1): bka' bstsald pa/sangs rgyas kyi ston pa chos yin mod kyi/chos dang sangs rgyas ma yin pa gzhan gis myi stond to//lam zhes bya ba yang myis sgrub dgos pas/de pas na sangs rgyas sngar smos so
193b10(1): de nas ma 'gags pas yang bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das dkon mchog gsum la skyabs su gsol dang gnod pa'i tshe/sngar chos dkon mchog smos la/phyis sangs rgyas dkon mchog
195a1(1): //gzhan ma smos so//gsold pa/so sor mthard ba'i khrims ni zag pa myed pa'i khrims lags sam/bsam gtan gyi khrims lags /bka' stsald pa /zag pa myed pa'i khrims kyang ma yin
195a2(1): bsam gtan gi khrims kyang ma yin no//so sor thard pa'i tshul khrims 'di ni /gal te sangs rgyas 'jig rten du gshegs na ni tshul khrims 'di yod/gal te sangs rgyas 'jig rtan du ma gshegs mtshul khrims 'di
195a3(1): myed do//zag pa myed pa dang/bsam gtan gi tshul khrims ni /sangs rgyas 'jig rten du gshegs kyang rung/ma gshegs kyang rung/dus thams cad kyi thams cad du yod do//so sor thard pa'i tshul
195a4(1): khrims ni bcas pa la yod par 'gyur gyi /zag pa myed pa dang /bsam gtan gi tshul khrimasani bcas pa las yod pa ma yin no//so sor mthard pa'i tshul khrims ni gzhan las thos bar 'gyur gyi /zag
195a5(1): pa myed pa dang/bsam gtam gi tshul khrims ni gzhan las/thos pa ma yin no//so sor thadad pa'i tshul khrims ni nyid kyis log gam/dge' pa 'am sdig pa 'am/lung du mi ston
195a6(1): pa'i sems kyis dus thams cad du yod do //zag pa myed pa dang bsam gtan gi tshul khrims ni bsam gtan la 'jug pa dang/zag pa myed pa'i sems la 'jug pa'i tshe/tshul khrims yod kyi
195a7(1): so sor thard pa'i tshul khrims ni myi'i nang 'ba' shig na yod kyi /zag pa myed pa dang/bsam gtan gi tshul khrims ni lha dang myi gnyis ka la yod do//so sor thard pa'i tshul khrims 'di ni /'dod ba'i khams 'ba'
195a8(1): shig na yod kyi /zag pa med pa dang/bsam gtan gyi tshul ni 'dod ba'i khams dang/zugs kyi khams ma rdzogs bar yod do//zag pa myed pa'i tshul khrims dang/so sor thard pa'i tshul khrimams ni sangs
195a9(1): rgyas kyi slob ma 'ba' shig la yod kyi /bsam gtan gi tshul khrimams ni mu stegs can la yang yod do//gsold pa/dge snyend gi khrims lnga la /du ni rang bzhin gi sdig pa lags /du ni bcas ba'i sdig pa
195a10(1): lags /bka' stsald pa/bzhi ni rang bzhin gi sdig/chad ni 'thung pa'i khrims gcig ni bcas pa'i sdig go//chad 'thungs pa ni bag myed pa'i le lo'i gnas yin te/sdig pa bzhi'i grogs su gyur
195b1(1): /khrims lnga la chang ('a)thung ba ni bag myed pa'i bzhi yin pas /khrims bzhi' yang nyams par byed do//dper na sangs rgyas 'od srung gyi tshe/dge' slong zhig 'chang ('a)thungs pa'i rkyend kyis/gzhan gi chung ma la 'dod pas

196a1(1): //pa'i bar du srog dang bcas pa thams cad las /tshul khrims 'di bzhi thob tu rung te/de yang thog ma tshul khrims nor ba'i tshe/srog thams cad myi gcod pa dang /thams cad ma byin par

197a1(1): /rdzogs pa 'am/bsam gtan gi tshul khrims sam/zag pa myed pa'i tshul khrims mnos kyang thams cad thob par myi rung ngo/gal te tshul khrims lnga nyams pa'i 'og tu khrims lnga

197a6(1): mchog yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/gal te sangs rgyas 'jig rtend du gshegs na/tshul khrims 'di yod par 'gyur gyi/bsam gtan gi tshul khrims dang/zag pa myed pa'i chul

197a8(1): tshul khrims ni sems can las thob pa yin no//so sor thard pa'i tshul/khrims ni sems can thams cad la byams pa'i sems kyis thob par 'gyur gyi/bsam gta gi tshul khrims dang/zags pa myed pa'i
197a9(1): tshul khrims na byams pa'i tshul khrims thob pa ma yin no//so sor thad pa'i tshul khrims ni sangs rgyas kyi chos 'dzind ba 'khor rnam bdun 'jig rtend na yod cing/theg pa rnam gsum gi 'bras bu rgyun myi
197a10(1): chad par gzhir gyurd pa yin gi/bsam gtan gi tshul/khrims dang/zag pa myed ba'i tshul khrims ni de lta bu ma yin te/de pas na khams gsum na mchog rab phul tu gyurd pa yin no//thog ma khrims nod pa'i tshe
197b1(1): gsol ba dang zhu ba bzhi'i las byas ma thag tu tshul khrims rdzogs par gyur te/thog ma'i sems skad cig ma dang po'i tshul khrims kyi rnam par rig byed kyi bzugs ni las shes kyang bya /las kyi lam zhes bya ba'o
